# Changelog

## [Unreleased]
- Any tab can hold HTML and PDF posts (Jonas, 25 Nov 2026). A `report` block whose `match` finds a standalone HTML page or a PDF now reads it beside report files and claims it from `artifacts`; opening an HTML or PDF post from the Changes panel goes to the tab whose `report` block holds it, else to the tab whose `artifacts` block lists it, and only then to the separate posts page. Opening an article already went to its tab (#2353).
- An agent's changes fold into one box above its chat (Jonas, 25 Nov 2026: "when there is enough happening in an agents thread, the sidepanel gets too chaotic"; design C on the canvas). Folded, the box is one line, "4 changes ready", and a down arrow; a click on it lists each change as one line with its preview switch, with Approve all (two-step, keeps its size) and an up arrow at the top. A click on a change opens it alone in place: its sentence, its article and Approve / Change / Discard; a single change opens by itself. Discard all is gone from the box (the agent card's right-click Remove in the overview does that). No agent's chat repeats a change as its "Staged change: …" line any more; every other note about a change (merged, sent, discarded, a merge conflict) still shows (`views/surface-session-drawer.ts`, `styles/surfaces.css`, four Danish entries).
- The Changes panel handles approvals better (Jonas, 25 Nov 2026): a change being approved or merging leaves its card at once, and a card with nothing left to act on goes away; the "Merging…" toast sits in the middle of the preview instead of its foot; "Approve all N" in the panel header approves every turned-on change across agents (not inbox Send sessions or agents still working) with one toast and one Undo (`SurfacesStore.approveChanges`, two-step green Confirm). Undo now restores every session it touched. One Danish entry.
- The rail's interfaces are one list with no seeded Building and Running groups; Inbox stands first by default (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026; 24 Nov: "you cannot delete a side-panel category for interfaces"). Each member drags interfaces to any place (Alt with an arrow moves the focused one), makes sections of their own (right-click the rail or an interface: New section, Move to) and renames, moves or deletes them from a section's title. The order and sections are the member's own in the cloud (`qorpera_member_surface_rail`, 20260930892000); Reset sidebar takes them away and Undo puts them back. Before that migration the rail keeps Inbox first and moves nothing.
- The rail is a tenth thinner and its Co-Founder a tenth smaller, with half the space down to the interfaces (Jonas, 24 Nov 2026). Default width 406 -> 365 design px (Small 502 -> 452; the stored width moves to `qorpera.web.rail-width.v6`, so everyone starts once from the new default; the Concepts side panel follows), figure 82 -> 74 with its lift 37 -> 33, and where the rail lists interfaces the Co-Founder's bottom padding 18.5 -> 1.25 px, so its card to the first section's words goes 34.5 -> 17.25 px. The boot placeholder's figure follows (76 -> 68).
- Removed the Builds on / Used by link indicator strip across all studio interfaces (Jonas, 24 Nov 2026: "I think the interfaces are only getting bloated with this indicator of the links - let's just remove them accross all interfaces"), along with its styles and unused Danish entries (`views/surface-studio-view.ts`, `views/surface-layout-view.ts`, `styles/surfaces.css`, `ui/danish/surfaces.ts`). Live Identity token styling and `/identity/...` file reads are unchanged.
- Preview-aware session lineage and chain toggling (migration `20260930890000_surface_preview_lineage.sql`): `SurfacesStore.startSession` captures currently visible preview change IDs when starting a session in Preview/Compare mode; Studio, Report, and `code_repo` previews automatically ensure background chain or standalone reconciliations via `lineageReconciliationTarget` and `activeReconciliationFor`, showing only the quiet `Updating preview…` pill while running and slotting in reconciled variants transparently when ready.
- Silent background reconciliation for overlapping interface changes (migration `20260930888000_surface_silent_reconciliation.sql`): removed founder-facing `Conflict` badges, banners, status labels, `Reconcile` / `Reconcile all` buttons, and visible `Reconcile:` sessions; Studio and Report views apply 3-way merges and cached ready overlays transparently, auto-trigger background reconciliation when needed, show a quiet `Updating preview…` indicator while reconciling, and fall back to `Couldn't update preview` + `Retry` only after reconciliation fails.
- Added PDF deliverable support in Studio `artifacts` blocks, Report view, and sidepanel deliverable pills (`views/surface-blocks/pdf.ts`, `views/surface-blocks/artifacts.ts`, `views/surface-report-view.ts`, `views/surface-session-drawer.ts`, `surfaces-store.ts`, `ui/danish/surfaces.ts`): inline PDF previews render through a dedicated same-origin `/pdf-preview/` door (`public/pdf-preview/`, `public/_headers`) that removes the inherited `/*` headers and scopes `frame-src blob:` (`object-src 'none'`, `frame-ancestors 'self'`) to that page alone while keeping `/*` free of `frame-src`, checks `event.source` and `location.origin` both ways on a one-shot `postMessage` handshake, and pins the Blob MIME type to `application/pdf` (with `Open PDF`, `Download`, Compare cards, and `scripts/test-web-pdf-preview-browser.mjs` Chromium proof).
- Renamed the Studio `files` layout block to `artifacts` (`views/surface-blocks/artifacts.ts`), removed the catch-all `Files` tab from Product and Identity starters so agents place `artifacts` sections directly on any tab, added raster image support (`.png`, `.webp`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`) alongside SVGs, docs, pages and JSON cards, removed raw `.studio-file-tag` filepaths from Studio block headers and Compare diff card headers (keeping paths only in Compare's `Other changed files` list), and added fullscreen viewing (`views/surface-blocks/fullscreen.ts`) for every `artifacts` item (with prev/next lightbox navigation across visual grids) plus `brand_mark`, `stage_3d`, `explainer`, `module` and `table` blocks.
- A website that was never published, or whose Base moved since its last publish, is published by itself while it is not live, as approved changes already were (Jonas, 24 Nov 2026: the website link did not show in the notch for any of his websites). Thetaband's Hjemmeside and Meditationsapp had a site but no published version, so the bar never showed their address. `#publishWhileNotLive` now also publishes when the summary's `hasUnpublishedChanges` says Base has something to publish (it already covers a Base never published), once per Base, and Go live publishes such a Base first. The bar is unchanged.
- The surface bar fits a phone (360-390 px wide) in every state (`styles/surfaces.css`, `views/surface-view.ts`, `views/surface-address-sheet.ts`): on a narrow bar the address shows only its mark also while its state is unread (a grey dot, its host as its name and tooltip), teammates' faces fold to one face and "+N", and on the narrowest bar the faces step aside when Publish is there, the Live / Preview buttons tighten, and Publish shortens its long states ("Publishing…" to its spinner and a muted "Publish", "Sofie said no" to her face and "No", no count while waiting for a teammate) so Publish and Live / Preview are never cut off.
- Added a Voice row directly below Coding & working in Settings → Default models (`views/settings-default-models.ts`, `cloud/default-models.ts`, `ui/danish/settings.ts`, migration `20260930886000_member_default_voice`) so members can pick their default voice model (Gemini 3.8 Flash TTS, Gemini 3.8 Flash-Lite TTS when a Gemini key is connected, or ChatGPT Voice) and voice profile from the profiles that model provides.
- A failed preview build no longer blanks the frame when the member has a preview that built before (step B3, migration `20260930889000`): `SurfaceDoorStage` shows that kept preview with a quiet pill, "Being fixed" while the session that broke the build has its fix turn and the failure sentence otherwise; with no kept preview, or before the migration is live (no `kept` field), the failure sentence shows as before. The store mints and keeps the kept preview's pass like a ready frame's. The publish sheet adds "Being fixed" under a failed publish's sentence while its fix turn runs (`publishBeingFixed`). One Danish entry.
- Added the native `explainer` layout block (`views/surface-blocks/explainer.ts`, `views/studio-explainer-engine.ts`) and a `Breakdowns` tab on both Product and Identity studios: matches `breakdowns/**/scenes.js`, loads the explainer engine as a hashed on-demand asset (`explainer-<hash>.js`), attaches sibling `voice/clips.json` audio binaries (`audio/wav`, `audio/mp4`, `audio/mpeg`, `audio/webm`) into `AppFrame` with Base/Preview diff markers, keeps mounted `AppFrame` sections in place across unrelated same-tab re-renders (`views/surface-studio-view.ts`), switches between multiple breakdowns in place, claims breakdown files out of `Files`, ranks `scenes.js` at priority 0 ahead of `*.report.json` and loose assets, and expands Studio folder listing to 3 levels, 32 subfolders, and 256 binary refs.
- A website (site kind `files`) shows and manages no password any more (Jonas, 23-24 Nov 2026: "let's just drop the password for looking at the offline page"; server side since migration `20260930881000`). Sites (`views/settings-sites.ts`): a website's row has no password pills and its menu no Change password; its badge says "Live" when everyone can open it and "Not live yet" otherwise, in place of the state word "Live", and a published website shows neither while the hosting read has not answered, and its menu item says "Go live" / "Make it not live", with toasts in the same words; the line under the title reads "Share a site with its link once it is live." (with "An app link also needs its password." while an app link is listed), and Take offline no longer says a website's password is deleted. A thread's site card (`views/thread-sites.ts`) has no copy-password key for a website, and a published website's card says "Live" (with its dot) only when everyone can open it, "Not live yet" when not, and nothing while that is unknown, following the company's `sitesPublic` / `sitesTakenDown` through the new `sitePublicAnswer` (`app/company.ts`, which the rail's `siteAnswer` now uses too). What's new (`views/whats-new.ts`) drops "The password is in Sites."; a website's live version is headed "Live now" only when everyone can open the site, "Not live yet" when not, with an owner's or admin's line "Only the company can open it. Go live on its page." linking in-app to the site's own interface (where Go live is), or the plain "Only the company can open it." when the company has no interface for it, and "Current version" while that is unknown, drawn again as the answer arrives or changes. App links keep their password, Make public / Make private and the Public badge unchanged.
- Removed Claude models (`Opus 5.5`, `Fable 5.2`) from the Conversational default model selector in Settings → AI subscriptions (`views/settings-default-models.ts`, migration `20260930882000_conversational_default_no_claude.sql`); Claude models remain available under Coding & working.
- Changes no longer list the files they touch (Jonas, 24 Nov 2026: "let's get rid of these file links in the agents boxes"). Under an agent's card and in the Changes drawer a change is its title, its switch and Approve; the file chips that linked to Files are gone, with their styles (`views/surface-session-drawer.ts`, `styles/surfaces.css`).
- The page rule comes from contracts (step A5): `surfaceLookOf` calls `surfaceLookKind`, the surface view picks the reading page by `surfacePageKind`, and the studio view picks its starter by `surfaceStarterKind` with `surfaceFilesHaveModel`, the rule the runtime now tells agents; `readSurfaceFolderFiles` moved to `@qorpera/cloud-client/surface-folder-files`. Nothing drawn changes.
- The website address card greys the platform's domain of an address on `qorpera.site` as it does on `qorpera.com` (`views/surface-address-sheet.ts`, by the shared `NATIVE_SITE_HOST` rule): `shop.qorpera.site` shows `shop` with a grey `.qorpera.site`, no longer `shop.qorpera` with a grey `.site`.
- Custom interfaces are drawn from the Custom starter layout on the same renderer as Product and Identity, and the old page code is gone (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "Custom surfaces become first-class again: same composable blocks + `module` escape hatch"). Every studio interface that is neither Product nor Identity (custom, brand, database, dashboard and the rest) and has no `layout.json` now shows Overview (its title with the one line or the pale line, then its files under "Files", whose quiet "Working files" line stays when the folder is empty) and Reports (its `*.report.json`, pale while there are none), in place of the whole-page "No studio files yet" and the old sections and tabs (Specs & BOM, Production & Vendors, Analysis, Brief & Files, Page(s)). A `.json` file's card in a `files` block now also shows its colours as swatches, its typefaces (an object of them or one `font` string) as type rows (first family, as on Identity) and lists of objects such as `vendors` as tables. The new `module` block (`views/surface-blocks/module.ts`) draws a small app from its folder's `index.html` in the same sandboxed page frame as a page card, with the folder's own stylesheets and scripts inlined. This changes what reaches the frame, for page cards too: `prepareStudioHtmlPreview` now writes the code of a `<script src>` from the folder into the page, so the page is no longer the text the safety check read. `frameParsedPage` therefore parses the changed page's text again and checks that second parse (`unfit`), and the page is not shown when anything turns up there; inlined code also has `<!--` written as `\x3c!--` and `</script` as `<\/script` (case kept), so it cannot open a comment or end its element. An inlined classic script with `defer` moves to the end of `<body>` in document order, and `defer` and `async` are dropped from inlined classic scripts; a module script stays where it was. `height` a number is that height, `auto` follows the app's own height (a short script appended to the app sets `height: auto` and `min-height: 0` on `html` and `body`, so heights tied to the frame cannot make it grow, then posts its height to the app window, which sets the frame between 120 and 1600 px), and a module alone on its tab fills the tab. An empty module shows the frame's dashed outline under its heading; a waiting change to any file in its folder marks it; a `files` block never lists its files. Studio folders are now read two folder levels deep, also when the root alone holds more than the 32-file cap, and `.js`/`.mjs` files are read and ranked with `.html`, so a module under `modules/<name>/` reaches Base with its scripts. Compare mode, the token bridge and the Builds on / Used by line are unchanged. Removed: the old page's parsing, tabs and sections, `.studio-report-link` and `.studio-vendor-certs` styles, and 12 Danish entries nothing uses.
- Identity is drawn from its starter layout on the same renderer as Product (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "Identity uses the exact same renderer with its starter homes"), and the Identity branch of the old page is gone. Overview shows the Logo and Wordmark each on a fixed light and dark ground (an `.svg` text file or a `.png`/`.webp`/`.jpeg` picture, read once and freed with the page), the company's name (`identity/company.json`, else the name the seed writes in `tokens.json`) with the voice line, Mission & Ambition as two named cards, the Palette and the Type set in each face; then Strategy, Philosophy (every report in the folder), Voice (`voice.md`, `brief.md`), Design system (the rest of `tokens.json`, such as radius and spacing, `design-system.json` and any other JSON file no block shows) and Files (every other note, picture and page). Each home is drawn before its file exists, and an Identity with no files shows them in place of the "No studio files yet" page. A company's own `identity/layout.json` is now honoured. New blocks `palette`, `brand_mark` and `type_specimen`; `cards` takes named cards; `files` takes `skip`; a page under Files is marked when a stylesheet or picture it links changed. A block on any other interface bound to `/identity/...` reads the company's Identity as the token bridge already composes it, waiting changes included (`SurfacesStore.companyIdentityFiles`). Identity's Used by line is the one every studio has (Builds on / Used by from the shared asset links). The report home reads "Reports and posts live here." on every interface.
- A website that is not live has only a preview (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "when a website is not yet live - there should only be a preview probably, no live version, since it's not live"). While its site is not public, or the company's site list says it has no served site yet, the bar (`views/surface-view.ts`) draws no Live / Preview switch and no Publish: only the address and the desktop / phone switch, and the view is always Preview; the "Merged" toast drops its Publish button there too. What a member who may write approves is the site: the bar quietly switches the interface's Auto-publish on and publishes what is already approved, once per count, with no toast or banner (`publish` and `setAutoPublish` take `{ quiet: true }` in `surfaces-store.ts`). While the app does not know whether the site is served (the site list is not read yet, or its last read failed: `siteListUnread`, `siteServed` in `company.ts`) or whether it is public, the bar draws neither and calls nothing; a failed read of whether it is public keeps the old bar. Go live in the address card (`views/surface-address-sheet.ts`, two optional hooks) first waits for any publish already running, then publishes what is approved and waits for that job itself to finish, for at most five minutes; meanwhile the button reads "Going live…" in the same box and the site is not shown as public. Only then is the site made public, and Auto-publish is switched off and kept off: a failed or late answer is corrected on the next change, quietly three times, then once with the usual error. If the publish fails or the wait runs out, the site stays not live with the card's own problem line. "Make it not live" switches Auto-publish back on. A public website the view did not take live keeps its Auto-publish as it is. On a narrow page the address keeps its name when it stands alone in the bar, and a bar whose only part is the hidden desktop / phone switch is not drawn (`styles/surfaces.css`). Studios and operations interfaces are unchanged.
- A failed preview or publish build is worded by its code line, never shown raw (`previewStatusText`, `publishFailureText`, `surface-door.ts`, `surface-publish-sheet.ts`): the build failed, the company is out of computer hours, no machine was free in time (try again in a moment), or the output does not fit the limits of a site; each with its Danish entry.
- Added specific Settings → AI subscriptions guidance for Google AI Studio's HTTP `402` / `$0` prepay balance gate (`prepay_balance_empty` in `cloud/gemini-key.ts`, `views/settings-gemini-key.ts`, `ui/danish/settings.ts`), explaining that a prepay balance above `$0` is required to resume service even when AI Ultra monthly credits are claimed, and updated the fallback `billing_required` copy in English and Danish.
- A Product studio is drawn from a layout instead of fixed page code (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "I do not want the surface code to hard-code the shape of each interface"). A new renderer (`views/surface-layout-view.ts`, blocks under `views/surface-blocks/`) reads the interface folder's `layout.json`, or the Product starter layout when the folder has none or it does not parse, and draws its tabs and blocks (`stage_3d`, `title`, `swatch_row`, `facts`, `spec_list`, `table`, `cards`, `report`, `files`, `row`; any other block a quiet empty home). Each block draws its own empty home and marks what a waiting change touched in its own values. Any interface with its own `layout.json` moves onto it too; Identity and every other interface keep their page. The fixed five tabs and the tagline guess are gone. Figures read from an object show only values of at most 18 characters, the first four that fit (a rule of the `facts` block); named figures always show, a long one cut short with an ellipsis. A `spec_list` draws lists of objects as tables under it. A `files` block shows only files no other block of the layout draws, `.json` files too (as a card of their values; never an `object.json` or a `.report.json`, which have blocks of their own), and keeps its quiet line only when it is alone on its tab or has a heading. The Product starter keeps what today's page gave: the line is read from the six places it was read from, the dots from `colorways`, `colors`, `palette` or `swatches`, the vendor cards from `vendors` or `suppliers` in their old order, Specs ends with "Other details" for the rest of the specs file (tables too) and a card for every other `.json` file, and the folder's own `.html` pages show under Files in place of a Pages tab. It narrows on purpose: values are read from `specs.json` and `vendors.json` (plus any other `.json` file as a card), no longer sniffed by key name from every file, so for example colours in another file no longer become dots. Each empty tab now shows its blocks' empty homes in place of one grey sentence.
- The website's address card is plain rows (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "it's unintuitive with all that text ... bringing your own domain should be a button, not a suggestion only when it's live"). The card opens on the address alone: no stops, no status, no sentences. Before the site is public it offers one full-width Go live, which asks twice the way Approve does (the first press turns it green and says Confirm at the same size), and a "Use your own domain" row; once public, "Open the site", "Copy link", the domain row and a quiet "Make it not live". "Use your own domain" opens a field with Connect, also before the site is public, and lands on that domain's by-hand page: one line, the DNS lines with a copy button each, "Copy as a message for someone else" and Remove. On the main page each domain is one row: "Add the lines" or "No longer points here" while it is not connected, a chevron to "Add another address" and Remove once it is. A connected domain becomes the address by itself (the card sets it primary once), so "Use this address" is gone, and the bar and the card show only an address that works. The bar marks a site that is not public with a hollow dot instead of the lock, and the amber waiting mark is gone. The card no longer offers the site's password; Settings > Sites keeps it.
- The search window is redrawn (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "sits too high on the page, has borders on the searchfield, doesn't have the hover glide when moving between items, has poor icons, a horribly unneccessary footer, disgusting subtitles for direct filepaths, no filters or categories, no time-indicators"). It opens a fifth of the way down the page, was an eighth. The field has no outline. Each hit is one line: its picture, its name, a few quiet words and how long ago it changed ("12m", "3h", "2d"). A file wears the Files page's glyph in its type's colour and says where it is in plain words ("Hjemmeside", "brand / logo"), never a path; the folders a code area keeps its copies in (`main/`, `worktrees/<id>/`) are never named, an agent's working copy reads "Hjemmeside · draft", a file shows once (its published copy, else its newest draft) and git's own files stay out. A setting wears its tab's icon. The highlight is the app's travelling tint and follows the pointer and the arrow keys (`HoverGlide` takes `picks` and `moveTo`). A picker left of the field narrows to All, Agents and people, Apps, Files or Settings and lists that kind with nothing typed; the button right of it sorts (Best match, Latest, Name) and shows only what Needs you, is Working, or is an image, a document or a folder. The key hints under the list and the "Open …" words on the picked row are gone (`views/search-window.ts`, `views/search-files.ts`, `styles/search-window.css`).
- The bar at the top of an interface is redrawn (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026, the bar settled after seven rounds). It rests closed as a small floating pill with the view's word (Live, Base, Notes or Preview) and the blue count; a click, Enter or Space grows it in place into one grey bar, and it shrinks back on a press outside it (inside the preview too), on Escape or when focus leaves it, never while the address sheet, the publish menu or another menu from it is open. Open, the view and the desktop / phone switches are two words or icons with a white thumb that slides between them, thin lines part the groups, and Publish is a plain word with its blue count. A website that is not public stays open, so its address is one click away, and the bar opens by itself when a teammate asks you to publish. The agents are no longer in the bar (the right panel shows them), the collapse arrow and dragging the closed tab along the top are gone (`views/surface-view.ts`, `styles/surfaces.css`, `ui/danish/surfaces.ts`). When the right panel covers the page, the bar centres in the part still showing. On a narrow bar the address shows only its mark and the device switch steps aside; what still does not fit scrolls sideways under a soft fade.
- The rail's Agents section drops its "Working" title, and the first row stands closer under Agents and under Members (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "remove the \"Working\" title to the section, and bring down the spacing between the agents title and the first agent. Same for members"). Agents at work are listed first with no title of their own; Scheduled and Ready keep theirs. The space between each title and its first row goes from 12px to 2px; the space after the last row and between the groups stays 12px.
- The publish window asks one thing (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "there is so much slop that I don't know what im looking at"). "Patch notes" is now "Publish 1 change?" over the interface's name and address, then each change as its title and what it does with a quiet Undo, then Publish. Gone from the window: the change-count badge, the share box (link, Copy link, Copy password, Open), the line about Base, the green dot, the session chip, the time, the approver when it is you, the frame around each change, Cancel (the cross, Escape and a press outside close it), and the count on the button. A change approved with others says "Undo with 2 others" in place of "Rollback batch (3)". With nothing left it reads "Nothing to publish" with Done. The site's link and password moved to where its address lives: the address sheet has "Copy link" beside "Open the site", and "Copy password" while only the team can open the site (owners and admins, as before). The Publish menu loses its "Patch notes" row (the Publish button opens the same window), and the toast after an approve offers "Publish".
- Aligned email sign-in, confirmation, and password-reset OTP codes to 8 digits end-to-end (`auth.ts`, `views/signin.ts`, `styles/app.css`, `ui/danish/workspace.ts`, `auth-email/*.html`, `supabase/config.toml`) to match live Supabase Auth's 8-digit OTP codes, with 8 digit boxes grouped 4+4 and auto-submit on the 8th digit.
- Improved Gemini API key health verification and error messages in Settings → AI subscriptions (`cloud/gemini-key.ts`, `views/settings-gemini-key.ts`, `ui/danish/settings.ts`): automatically runs the vault's live `gemini-4-neon` check after saving a key, shows specific billing/Free Tier (`limit: 0`)/spend-cap/daily-quota/rate-limit/key-restriction/region messages from optional closed `errorDetail` values during live checks in the current Settings view (falling back to the persisted `lastErrorCode` message after reload or from failed chat runs) in English and Danish, and confirms readiness once the inference check succeeds.
- An Approve or Send button keeps its size on its second step (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "it should stay the same size, just turn green and say confirm"). In the session panel and the overview, Approve, Send, "Approve all" and "Send all" no longer shrink or grow when they turn into the green Confirm: the button holds both labels in one place (`approveLabel` in `views/surface-session-drawer.ts`, the unseen one as `.surface-approve-face.is-ghost`), so its width is the wider of the two in both steps. The check mark on the green step is gone; the small Send pill in the overview is wider at rest so Confirm fits. In the dark theme the bordered buttons now turn green too (the theme's `.btn` fill used to win over the green).
- Agents and Members stand on the rail without a card (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "these cards shouldn't sit in such an obviously different section. they should be more silent"). The window at the rail's foot loses its border, shadow and rounded box. Its two titles line up with the section names above and stand a little closer together, and their arrows are a shade lighter. The interfaces fade out under it instead of being cut by an edge, and stay clickable there; its own rows fade at its top and foot when it scrolls. It still stays in view while the interfaces scroll and still scrolls by itself.
- A Product studio draws its real 3D model (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "a real 3D model to orbit and take apart", after the hand-drawn one read as 2D clipart). When the studio folder holds a `.glb` (the one `object.json` names, else `model.glb`, else the first), the Overview stage shows it in studio light with a soft floor shadow: drag to turn and tilt, a slow turn by itself (none with reduced motion), a double-click turns it back, Whole / Apart eases its parts out from the middle (the `parts` of `object.json`, each with an optional `pull`, else its top-level pieces) and the colour dots recolour the materials `object.json` names in its new `tint`. The Object kit's 3D library is fetched once, only when a stage first needs it; while the file arrives, and where it cannot be read or 3D cannot run, the stage is the empty floor. `readSurfaceFolderFiles` now also lists a studio's `.glb`, `.webp`, `.png` and `.jpg` files by reference (`SurfacesStore.readStudioBinary` reads one, kept by its hash), the app's `connect-src` admits `blob:` for a model's own textures, and the local sandbox's Product holds a small headband model. Without a `.glb` the studio is as before.
- The figure grid answers the pointer (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "we are missing a hover animation when hovering over an avatar. if the background could be highlighted, and the avatar updating at lighting speed"). In the rail's right-click Figure panel and in the New surface window, the sliding hover tint follows the pointer from figure to figure, and the interface's own avatar (its rail row, or the window's large avatar) shows the hovered figure at once; moving off the grid or closing it puts the saved one back. A picked figure stays on the rail row while it saves, in place of appearing when the server answers, and the old one returns only if the save fails. `surfaceFigureChoices` takes a `preview`; a previewed figure is drawn with eager loading and sync decoding so its picture and eyes appear in the same frame.
- Deleting an interface stops its work (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "Yes, build it"). The server deletes the interface's scheduled agents and stops its sessions in the same step as the interface (`qorpera_archive_surface`, migration `20260930875000_surface_delete_stops_work`), so `SurfacesStore.deleteSurface` is one call, the scheduled agents are no longer read first, and a failure leaves the interface and every scheduled agent as they were; the rail's two "… scheduled agents was deleted before that" messages are gone. Needs that migration live.
- The rail's People is called Members and folds like Agents (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026): it starts folded with the count of members, opens and closes from its title, and remembers the choice on this device. It stays open while a member's chat is open or a member's message waits, until it is folded by hand; it opens by itself while a member is dragged.
- Confirm dialogs read cleaner (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026, on the dialog that deletes an interface: "this needs to look cleaner. I like the box size, but use bigger text, no subtitle, and filled background for the delete button"). The question is larger (17px, its two lines balanced), the buttons a little taller, and a dialog's red answer is filled (`--danger-solid`, white label) in place of red text in an outline; the box keeps its width. This is the app's one confirm dialog (`confirmDialog`, class `confirm-dialog`), so deleting an agent, a group chat, a section or a company looks the same. Deleting an interface asks its one question and no longer lists what goes with it underneath: the scheduled agents are still deleted with it, and its website is taken offline with it (migration `20260930885000`); the site and its address stay in Settings → Sites.
- A website interface's address in the bar opens a small sheet that takes the site through three stops: Only you, Live and Your domain (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "this looks good enough for the domain selection"). The address shows while the site is private too, with a lock; "Put it live" and "Back to only you" act on one press; a domain is added in one field, waits in one quiet line, shows its DNS lines with copy buttons only when asked, and offers "Use this address" once it points here. A refusal reads as one plain sentence; a member who is not a founder or admin sees the stops read-only. Settings builds its DNS lines from the same helper (`siteDnsRecords`).
- "Use this one" keeps the option (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "the multiple choice is supposed to live in the agents changelist on the right sidepanel though, while the preview always projects the page itself"). In Changes, a session's options end in "Use this one" and "None of these". "Use this one" takes one press, no confirm: the other options go, the chosen one goes on as a plain session with its changes still waiting (toggle, Approve, Change as for any session), its note stays with it, the page keeps showing it, and nothing is merged, so no Undo is offered. "None of these" moves the cursor to the page's own ask field ("What should it be instead?"); sending there starts a new session and drops the options, and leaving the field empty keeps them. Needs migration `20260930874000` (choosing no longer approves).
- Agents and People are a window of their own at the foot of the rail (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026): it stays in view while the interfaces scroll under it, and its own rows scroll inside it once Agents is open or the team is long. Each window lights its own rows on hover, and the selection slides only between the interfaces' rows.
- In Changes, each option of a session's ways to go says what it does on a line under its name (its first open change's summary), and a session counts as an option only while its choice is open: once one is chosen, it is listed, previewed and counted like any other session (in the panel, the bar, the rail and the page), so a chosen option whose changes are still waiting never drops out of sight (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "the multiple choice is supposed to live in the agents changelist on the right sidepanel"; groundwork for picking without merging).
- A website is its interface's page (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "it shouldn't sit in a container inside the interfaces page, it should BE the interface page"). In Live the site fills the whole page, as it already did in Preview: the framed window with its own address bar, Reload, open-in-new-tab and the visitor numbers beside it are gone from the interface. The address moved into the floating bar, before Live / Preview; a click opens the site in a new tab. The numbers stay on the website's own page under Sites.
- A Product studio keeps every home in place and reads as one quiet column (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "love it, let's build it!", after the Overview tab vanished from a studio that held only Markdown). Overview, Specs, Vendors, Reports and Files are always there, the empty ones in a lighter tone with one line saying what lives in them. Overview is the model alone on its row with a Whole / Apart switch, then the name, one line (`tagline` in `specs.json`, else the interface's brief), colour dots that recolour the model, and up to four short figures from the key specs; before any file exists the same layout shows with a soft floor, pale dots and "?" figures instead of the "No studio files yet" page. The full key specs moved to Specs, vector files to Files; the model's title row, named colour chips, Wireframe and Reset view are gone (a double-click turns the model back).
- An interface can be deleted from the rail (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "you are not able to delete an interface - let's add that (with a delete, then confirm button)"). A right-click on its rail row ends in a red Delete; the app's confirm dialog asks "Delete <name>? It is removed for everyone in the company." and deletes on its red Delete. Under the question the dialog lists what goes with it and what stays, read fresh when Delete is chosen: its scheduled agents are deleted too (a scheduled agent whose interface is gone would otherwise run on another one), its active sessions and unpublished changes go with it, sessions still working run to their end, and its website is taken offline with it (since migration `20260930885000`; the site and its address stay in Settings → Sites). `SurfacesStore.deleteSurface` reads the scheduled agents again, deletes those aimed at the interface, then archives it (`qorpera_archive_surface`); when that read fails nothing is asked or deleted, and a failure keeps the interface and says so in the rail, with how many of its scheduled agents were already deleted. Deleting the interface that is open goes to the company's page. The built-in ones (Inbox, Money, Files, Databases) have no Delete: the New surface window cannot make one again.
- Two people in one interface session (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "yes to all five, build it"): while a session works or asks, its figure in the rail, the surface bar and the panel wears the small face of the member whose turn it is; every member's message in the panel shows their face; a question's answer shows who answered. A message that starts with "@" and a teammate (picked from the list the "@" opens) is a quiet note to them: it never wakes the agent or reaches it, shows as a dashed bubble with their face, and puts "@" on the interface in their rail and on the session in the surface bar until they open it (`qorpera_surface_session_note`, `qorpera_surface_unseen_notes`, `qorpera_surface_notes_seen`, migration 866000).
- A member can ask a teammate to publish an interface instead of publishing now (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "yes to all five, build it"). Patch notes offers "Ask Sofie" (or a pick of the teammates who may publish) unless Auto-publish is on; the asked teammate gets yes and no on the Publish control with the preview in front of her and "Needs you" on the rail row, and yes is her own Publish. A no shows the asker "Sofie said no"; the asker can take an ask back, and it closes when anyone publishes or nothing is left (`qorpera_ask_surface_publish`, migration 20260930867000).
- People at the foot of the rail replace the Members page (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: multiplayer in place). Each teammate shows their face and where they are now ("On Website", "With Bo", "In Launch"); one click takes you there, or to their chat while a message of theirs waits, and their menu has Go to and Message before Pin and Hide. People ends with Invite, and the company menu has Invite people: both open the new-company invite sheet, where a press invites a friend as a builder or a spectator, or someone by email as a spectator. Who each agent works for, each member's ChatGPT (Connect, Sign in again, when it resets), moves and their asks, the Co-Founder for everyone and agents nobody works for move to Settings → Team. Migration `20260930869000` stops giving companies the Members surface and archives it; until it is live the web leaves it out.
- Automations keep an owner (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026, multiplayer in place): each Scheduled row in the rail wears its owner's small face, and its owner's login pays for its runs. Right-click offers "Hand to <teammate>" (to its owner or an owner or admin), and "Take the offer back" while it waits; the teammate sees the automation offered with Yes and No, the Agents section stays open for it and the tab counts it. A no shows as "Sofie said no". One whose owner left shows Nobody and does not run; any member takes it with Take it. One whose owner has no login that pays says "Paused: no model account pays for it" (`qorpera_offer_surface_automation`, `qorpera_answer_surface_automation_offer`, migration 868000).
- In Safari, the sliding hover tint in the rail and menus no longer flickers onto an item the pointer already left when the pointer moves fast (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026). `HoverGlide` moves the tint itself on each frame, easing toward the hovered item and keeping its own position, in place of restarting a Web Animation and reading the tint's on-screen box mid-flight.
- In Safari, the rail's sliding highlight no longer flashes over the figure and text of the item it travels to: every rail row keeps its own layer, so the highlight stays underneath while it moves (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026).
- Lowered overall CPU, GPU, and memory footprint across the web app: `Store.set` and `SurfacesStore` skip no-op re-emissions and idle 30-second rebuilds of unchanged cloud files/workspaces/automations while LRU-capping cached `cloudSurfaceFiles` (6 surfaces) and clearing module state on teardown; `avatar-appearance.js` computes PNG data URLs lazily instead of synchronously on every 512x512 figure plate and drops decoded source image references; `figure-motion.ts`, `figure-levels.ts`, and `figure-work.ts` skip static idle figures in the 60 Hz loop, pool scratch canvases by size, cap VRAM surface/mipmap caches with LRU eviction, release canvas backing stores on dispose, defer fallback image loads to errors, and eliminate `shadowBlur` on small rail figures; `HoverGlide`, `browser-bar.ts`, `uiScale()`, `crown.ts`, `conversation.ts`, and `shell.ts` cut per-pixel layout thrashing, sidebar MutationObserver walks, sign-in SVG blur rAF ticks, and agent-hub canvas rebuilds; CSS removes animated/nested/fullscreen `backdrop-filter` blurs, stops idle surface-figure/emblem animations, replaces non-composited `width`/`box-shadow`/`background-position` keyframes with `transform`/`opacity`, and scopes `.scroll-quiet` masks to scrolled containers; and memory leaks in `search-window.ts`/`shell.ts` (company retention), detached `ComputerPanel`s (`MachineScreenView`/`ComputerScene` canvases + detached panel cap), hidden `live_build` `SiteView` mounts, and `interaction-card.ts` image caches are fixed.
- The Files page takes over the Storage window's file window and Recents (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "Yes, add both"). A file opens in its own window over the page that grows out of its icon and shrinks back into it, in place of the preview beside the list; and a Documents | Recents switch beside the search replaces the Recent changes clock: Recents lists the company's changes grouped by day, searchable, and a changed file opens in its folder.
- Settings no longer has a Storage section (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: the Files page now looks like it). Settings is one window again: search, Friends, Close. Everything that opened Storage opens the Files page at the same file or folder: a folder link in a message, "Show in Files" (was "Show in Storage") on an app's page and on a concept's row, a letter's documents, and old `#/c/<id>/file` links. The Files page shows under its list what the company's storage holds in total ("164 MiB in total").
- Decoupled the surrounding Qorpera shell from heavy interface content so switching between large interfaces (including Product/Studio pages and large repository folders) no longer freezes the rail or app shell: `SurfaceStudioView` and `SurfaceReportView` now parse files and stream sections/HTML frames across <=6 ms main-thread slices via `ui/time-slice.ts`, `SurfaceStudioView` drops double `DOMParser` runs and caps oversized spec tables (100 rows) and plain-text files (32 KB) with on-demand expand buttons, `Studio3DViewer` coalesces renders via `requestAnimationFrame`, `readSurfaceFolderFiles` skips generated directories (`node_modules`, `.git`, `dist`, etc.) with bounded subfolder concurrency and a 1 MiB decoded byte cap, `RailSelection` and `HoverGlide` animate GPU-composited `translate`/`scale` transforms, `.surface-*` containers use CSS `contain` and `content-visibility: auto`, outgoing `SurfaceView` teardown is split into instant `deactivate()` plus idle `deferOffFrame` disposal, and `Origin-Agent-Cluster: ?1` is sent by `public/_headers` and `site-serve` to isolate framed `*.qorpera.com` sites into separate browser processes.
- Search is a window now (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026): the rail's magnifier, or ⌘K / Ctrl+K anywhere, opens one window over the app that finds agents (hidden and folded ones too, and by their section's name), members, group chats, apps, files across the whole Company File (by name, `qorpera_member_folder_search`, migration 864000) and settings (opening the right tab with the row lit). Empty, it shows what was opened from it lately and recently changed files. ↑↓ move, Enter opens, Tab narrows to one kind, Esc widens then closes; on a phone it fills the screen with a close button. The fold-out field over the company name and the rail's own filter are gone.
- Switching between interfaces in the rail now enters the target page immediately with a tailored breathing loading skeleton (`surfaceSkeleton` in `ui/skeleton.ts`) and defers heavy content mounting until after the first paint, cancelling mounts cleanly if the member switches away mid-load. Preview and report modes no longer mount hidden live site views underneath, `SurfaceStudioView` and `SurfaceReportView` keep existing DOM and HTML frames across unrelated workspace updates, `Studio3DViewer` defers its initial 3D canvas rasterization off the entry frame, `SurfaceRail` updates active selection in place without recreating rail rows or figure tiles, and ops Databases views dispose their watches when switching away.
- The composer at rest is a little more solid on surface pages and in conversations opened from the surface rail: its fill rises from 38% to 48% of the page colour in both themes (hover and focus unchanged at 80%; the thread-foot pills keep their 38% glass) (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026).
- The rail's Co-Founder figure no longer has a ground shadow under it, in light and dark (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026).
- Rail items (rows, pins, Co-Founder, interfaces, agent hub) no longer shrink while pressed; a click opens at once (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026).
- The rail's Agents section (Working, Scheduled, Ready) folds and opens from its title and starts folded (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026). Folded, the title shows only how many rows it holds and a chevron, in the title's own grey: no coloured badge, since the folded section should not draw the eye (Jonas, 23 Nov 2026: "the whole point is that it shouldn't draw your attention"); screen readers hear "Agents: 4". The choice is kept in this browser (`qorpera.web.rail-agents-open`). While the open page is an agent listed in it, the section shows open without changing the kept choice.
- Active interface and agent rows in the rail now follow the Appearance colour chosen in Settings (`--rail-selection`), matching the Co-Founder card and sliding the highlight between them, and the Settings Grey choice replaces dark `#5b5b5b` with the soft 7% text-mix surface grey (`#efefef` in light, `#2e302f` in dark) with readable dark/light foreground and ink tokens (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026).
- Surface figures in the rail no longer play the "!" / attention animation when a surface needs attention (they stay their own figure, resting or breathing while sessions run; screen-reader text still notes attention), and every surface with active sessions shows its mini agent figure row as the default subtitle whether open or not, hydrated and refreshed quietly in the background (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026).
- Removed the Company Files folder reference footer from the bottom of the right surface sidepanel (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026).
- Removed the History row under an open surface in the rail (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026); Members still lists its teammates underneath, and merged changes still roll back from Patch notes.
- An open surface in the rail shows its sessions as a row of small agent figures in place of its subtitle (six at most, then "+n"; each keeps its working, needs-you and merged look), and no longer lists the sessions underneath (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026). The surface page's agents bar opens each session and now carries the hand-off highlight (as does the row); Members keeps its teammates and History keeps Revert. The compact rail shows no session figures any more; the agents bar covers it.
- A member changes a surface's figure from the rail: right-click it, Figure, and pick (needs migration `20260930856000_surface_set_figure` live). The create sheet and this menu share one picker.
- Surfaces are drawn as figures in the agents' clay style (Jonas, 22 Nov 2026: "love all of them, let's ship them, and then let you change the avatars, but just not the colors"): each is the thing it is, in that thing's own fixed colours, with the agents' pill eyes (`ui/surface-figure.ts`, 26 pictures in `public/assets/surfaces/figures-v1/`). One figure per kind by default; in the rail it breathes and looks toward its sessions while they work and turns into "!" when it needs you. The create sheet picks a figure (icon `figure:<id>`) in place of the colour swatches and emblems; a surface's colour now follows its kind.
- Enabled `*.qorpera.site` own hosts, Look First preview links (`*.qorpera.site/.qorpera/enter`), and in-app Sites iframe door (`public/site-door/door.js`, `public/_headers`, `cloud/sites.ts`, `cloud/releases.ts`) alongside `*.qorpera.com`, added `recheckDomain` (`qorpera_recheck_company_site_domain`) with a Check DNS button, clickable verified custom domain links, and `Webapp`/`Hybrid` badges in `views/settings-sites.ts` (Danish translations in `ui/danish/settings.ts`).
- Added native web hosting client (`cloud/site-hosting.ts`) and Sites UI controls (`views/settings-sites.ts`, Danish translations in `ui/danish/settings.ts`) for `qorpera.site` public/private site visibility toggles and custom apex/subdomain management with DNS record guides (`A`/`AAAA`/`CNAME` + `_qorpera` `TXT`), backed by migration `20260930832000_native_web_hosting.sql`, `crates/qorpera-web-router`, and `crates/qorpera-app-host`.
- Support for Gemini 4 Neon (`gemini-4-neon`) in conversations (needs migration `20260930823000_gemini_4_neon` and the shared vault/runtime bundle that carries `geminiChoice: "thread"`). Settings → AI subscriptions adds a Gemini API key card (`settings-gemini-key.ts`, `cloud/gemini-key.ts`) where a member saves their own API key directly to the shared vault in two hops (never through Supabase), toggles "Use Gemini 4 Neon by default for my chats", sets or clears a monthly USD spend cap ($1–$100,000), and tracks current UTC month spend. The conversation Model menu (`composer-models.ts`, `cloud/session-model.ts`) cycles across OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google when offered, supports `low`/`medium`/`high`/`xhigh` effort on Gemini 4 Neon, hides Fast on Google, and shows a cap-reached note when the cap is hit. Company notices (`execution-notices.ts`, `cloud/member-notifications.ts`) display and dismiss `gemini_cap_reached` notices. Danish translations included.
- Agent messages a member did not ask for fold into one quiet line in the agent's own thread (thread decisions step 5; Jonas, 20 Nov 2026: "Fold them"; needs migration `20260930822000` live, and until then every message is drawn as before). A message is "not asked for" when its run was woken by a coworker's message, a worker's report, a machine event or a routine (`run_wake` `agent` or `routine`); a run a member's post or card answer woke, and an agent's first run, stay full size. One such message shows as its first sentence, grey, on one line; several in a row, with nothing of the member's or a card between them, show as "3 updates · " and the newest one's first sentence, and the chips of the coworker messages between two of them fold with them (a chip before the first one stays). A tap opens the whole messages under "Hide 3 updates" (they stay open, also when another arrives, until the member folds them or leaves the thread); a tap folds them again. Never folded: a message whose run raised a question or an approval card, an erased or held message, anything in a group chat, and any message with no `run_wake`. Correction (21 Nov, seen live after v270): history folds too. The wake of every past run is on record and the thread read adds it when it reads, so messages from before the migration carry `run_wake` and fold like new ones; only a message of a run with no wake on record stays as it was. Danish: "3 opdateringer", "Skjul 3 opdateringer".
- Opening a company no longer fills the browser console with "Applying inline style violates the following Content Security Policy directive 'style-src 'self''" (seen 20 Nov 2026: about 88 per page load in a company with nine interface apps). Since #1666 (11 Nov) the app reads every app's page ahead when the company opens and again after its folder changes (`warmApps`), and Chrome holds even a page parsed off screen (`DOMParser`) to the app's own policy, which refuses inline styles: every `<style>` and `style=` of every app's page logged one error, and again when the page's style was rewritten. Nothing a member sees was missing: the parsed copy is never shown, and an opened app runs in its own frame, whose policy allows its styles. Reading ahead now parses the page with its `<style>` elements read as `<noframes>` (raw text like a style, in the head and the body) and its `style` attributes under another name, and changes nothing in that copy, so it still reads the same files (the stylesheets, and the pictures their `url()`s name) and logs nothing. Opening an app or a page parses it exactly as before, so it can still log these errors for the page's own styles while it opens.
- A question an agent asks again after a member's note shows as the next round of one decision (thread decisions step 4; needs migration `20260930821000` live and agents on the bundle that carries `asksAgain`; until then no card has a round and nothing changes). While such a card waits, a small "Round 2" mark stands over its title with the words that led to it: "You said: “Warmer, with more motion”" to the member who typed them, "{member} said: “…”" to everyone else. In the Co-Founder chat's dock the round comes with the company's list (`round`, read only on a question whose payload names the card it asks again); in the agent's own thread it is read from the cards there, by following `asksAgain` back (at most 20). An answered card shows no round mark. Danish: "Runde 2", "Du sagde", "{member} sagde".
- An agent's first short words in a run ("Checking the refund log.") are no longer a message that stays in the thread (thread decisions step 5; Jonas, 20 Nov 2026: "Replace it": "A quiet status line at the agent's figure while it works. It goes away when the real answer comes, and nothing stays in the thread."). While that run works, the words stand as one quiet line beside the agent's working figure (under the window while it uses its computer), as plain text, in place of the hover caption; assistive technology hears "Nova is working. Checking the refund log.". When the run says more (an answer, a later progress message, a question or an approval card) the words are gone and nothing of them stays. They are the agent's own sentence, never a step or a command: a first message with a line break, with code marks, longer than 160 characters or with a file is not taken as first words and stays the message it is. If the work ends with nothing more, the words stay as the small message they were, so a stopped or failed run leaves its trace; they show as that message five seconds after the work is seen to have ended, and words that arrive a moment before their run is seen wait as long, because the agent's state and the thread are read apart and the words would otherwise flash as a message. In a group thread, which has no working figure, the words show as that small message until the run says more (one that still has earlier messages to read draws them as today: with several agents' runs side by side, a run's first loaded message is not known to be its first). "Says more" is by run: question and approval cards now carry the run that raised them (`runId`), so another agent's message or a worker's approval card in the same thread never counts. A typed note's "reworking" line now stays while the first words are all the agent has said. Web only; the agents' instruction and the kernel's rule (a final send needs an earlier progress send) are unchanged.
- Another member can send the pick of a decision asked of someone else (thread decisions step 3; Jonas, 20 Nov 2026: "Yes, with clear words": "Any member with a login may send it. The button first says the work will run on their login, and they press it themselves. Nothing ever switches by itself."; needs migrations `20260930819000` and `20260930820000` live). In the Co-Founder chat's dock, under another member's question, once that member has voted for an option, a member who has a usable model login of their own (never a spectator, never a member without one) sees the words "The work then runs on your login. The agent's next questions about this will come to you, and your approval rules apply." and a button "Send {name}'s pick: {option}". No vote, no button; the button follows the vote, and takes no tap for 600 ms when the vote changes under it. A press is held five seconds with Undo like any pick, stands in the chat as "{name}'s pick", and then goes as the member's own answer on their own login (`qorpera_answer_interaction` with exactly that option). If the asked member changed their vote meanwhile the cloud refuses it, the votes are read again at once and the card says "Nothing was sent. {name}'s pick is not that option now. Look at their vote again."; if the vote turns out unchanged (the cloud refused for another reason) the card shows the cloud's own sentence instead. In a thread an answer sent this way reads "{name}'s pick, sent by {sender}" ("Your pick, sent by {sender}" to the member who was asked, "{name}'s pick, sent by you" to the sender). A member who has NO usable login gets the other half: on a question asked of THEM a tap is their pick, not an answer (it could not be sent on a login they do not have), the option wears the ring, a second tap takes it back, and under the card it says "You have no login here that can answer, so a tap is your pick, not an answer. A member who has one can then send your pick; tap it again to take it back." (only on a question that has options to tap) A member with a login answers their own questions by a tap as before. Whether the member has a usable login is read without choosing an account when the company's list is read: again at most every 10 seconds while they have none, so a login connected meanwhile turns their own question back into one a tap answers, and every 5 minutes while they have one. Until a first read has come back their own question is drawn as always; a later read that fails changes nothing. A pick they left on their own question while they had no login stays in sight once they can answer: under the card stands "Your pick stands: a member with a login can send it. Answer here yourself, or take it back." with a button "Take my pick back"; nothing is taken back for them. A card that turns between the two (a pick card and an answer card) takes no tap for 600 ms, either way.
- Every member sees the company's open decisions in the Co-Founder chat's dock, and can vote and leave a note on the ones another member answers (thread decisions step 3; Jonas, 18 Nov 2026: "at least all users should be able to see the decision, who owns it - and maybe then be able to vote on it, without sending the decision"; needs migration `20260930819000` live, and shows the member's own decisions as before until then). The dock reads the company's list (`qorpera_list_company_open_decisions`) and falls back to the member's own list, then to the questions list, where the cloud has none yet (one refused call each, once each time the company is opened in the tab: a page load, or leaving the company and coming back). The member's own decisions come first; another member's stand after them, their tabs with a dashed edge. Another member's question is drawn by the same card, headed "{agent} asks {member}", with its options and no field and no Skip: a tap on an option is a vote, a second tap takes it back, and the option the member voted for wears a ring. Every option shows the faces of the members who voted for it, on the member's own questions too, where a tap still decides. Under the card stand the members' notes, and on another member's decision a field to add one (Enter saves it; `qorpera_cast_decision_vote`). A vote answers nothing, wakes no agent and needs no model account. Faces and notes are painted onto the card that stands there, so an answer half typed is not lost when a vote arrives. A spectator sees the options, the faces and the notes, with nothing to press. Votes go to the cloud one at a time in the order tapped, and a vote stays in sight through a read that began before it. When the decision on screen leaves by something other than the member's own doing (another member answered it), the dock goes on to the next of the other members' decisions if there is one, and a card that comes on stage by anything but the member's own choosing (a tab, Undo) takes no tap for the first 600 ms: the next card after another member's left, the next card after the member's own pick or sent email draft (a second quick tap answers nothing), a card that arrives in a dock that stood empty (not the cards the dock opens with), and a card that turns from a vote into the member's own to answer while it stands there. So a tap meant for one card cannot land as an answer on another. A vote the dock cannot read is left out without losing the list. A note being typed on another member's decision is kept, with the cursor in it, when the card is drawn again for a refused vote or its retry. Another member's approval is shown with nothing to answer and takes a note; another member's email draft is not shown yet. The rail's number and the questions dock on app pages count and offer only the member's own decisions, as before.
- Gmail drafts stand in the Co-Founder chat's decisions dock beside questions and approvals (thread decisions step 2; needs migration `20260930818000` live, like approvals). A draft an agent wrote for the member is drawn by the thread's own email card, with its editor: From, To, Cc, Subject, the letter, Send email, Discard, Save to Gmail. In the dock the letter's field takes the room the window leaves and the card may be taller than a question, up to 70 % of the window, so the buttons stay in sight; the field never goes under three lines, and in a window too short for that the card scrolls, with the fade at its lower edge (the fade now also follows a card that draws itself again, as the email card does once it knows the sender). Send email and Discard act at once, as in the thread: an email cannot be taken back, so there is no five-second hold and no Undo. A sent or discarded draft then stands in the chat as a block, "Email sent" with its subject and "To …", or "Discarded" with "Nothing was sent". Save to Gmail leaves the draft where it stands. A draft whose send was not confirmed, or whose agent has not been told yet, stays in the dock with the card's own words and its Refresh or "Retry notifying agent", also once the list no longer names it, past its time, and in the next dock; the rail's number counts it while it stands there. An email card whose send is unconfirmed keeps its Refresh after the draft's time has passed, in the dock and in a thread (it used to lose every button there, with nothing left to press); Send, Discard and Save stay off. In the dock the sender stays on one line (cut with "…" when longer than the card), so the rows above the letter keep their height. These layout rules hold for the draft alone: the approval of an email, which wears the same card, shows every recipient and its whole text in the dock as before. The model account for the draft's thread is chosen right before Send, Discard or Save, and before a retry of the agent's notice, never by looking. A draft that stands in the dock is not drawn a second time in the Co-Founder's own thread; both share one editor, so words typed in the dock are there when the thread draws it. Drafts for other mail services are not listed.
- An answer the cloud refuses because the member is not in the agent's thread says so in plain words (thread decisions step 2; the decisions list also shows an owner or admin the approvals of agents whose thread they are not in, and answering one would start that agent's work, which only the thread's members may do). The dock already put the card back, open, when its answer was refused, with the cloud's own sentence under it: "Only a member of this thread can wake its agent". It now reads "Your answer did not go. It would start this agent's work, and only members of the agent's thread can do that. Ask someone in that thread to answer." (Danish too), in the dock and in the thread alike; the block in the chat goes and the card's buttons work again, as before.
- The rail counts the decisions that wait for the member (thread decisions step 2; Jonas, 18 Nov 2026, on gathering decisions at the Co-Founder). The Co-Founder's card in the rail carries a small number at the figure's shoulder: how many decisions stand in its chat's dock for this member (questions today; approvals too once migration `20260930818000` is live). Its name for a screen reader says "3 decisions wait for you". No number with nothing to decide. A decision just picked in the dock leaves the count at once and counts again on Undo. The list behind it is now read on every page of an open company, not only while a dock is on screen: on the hints it already followed, and every 30 seconds while the page is visible.
- Approvals stand in the Co-Founder chat's decisions dock beside the questions (thread decisions step 2; needs migration `20260930818000` live, and shows questions alone until then). The dock reads the member's open decisions of both kinds in one list (`qorpera_list_member_open_decisions`): an approval is drawn by the approval card's own code (what the agent wants to do, the change, why it waits, Deny, Always allow…, Allow once; an email to send as the email approval card), with a tab under it like any decision. Its answer is held five seconds with Undo like a pick, then goes through `qorpera_answer_action_review` as in its thread, and stands in the chat as a block reading "Allowed once", "Always allowed" (with the rule) or "Denied". An approval that stands in the dock is not drawn a second time in the Co-Founder's own thread. While the cloud has no such list yet, the dock reads the questions list as before (one refused call, then no more).
- A decision made in the Co-Founder's chat can be taken back for five seconds, and then stands in the chat as a block of its own (thread decisions step 2; Jonas, 18 Nov 2026: "would like it to feel like a section more than a part of a long stream of messages"; 20 Nov: "Yes, build it"). A pick, a typed note or Skip in the decisions dock is held five seconds before it goes to the asking agent; meanwhile the next decision comes up and the chat shows a centred block where the decision was made: a thin rule with "Decided", "Your note" or "Skipped", the asking agent's figure and the question in small type, the answer in large words, and "Goes to {agent} in a moment" with Undo and its ring. Undo sends nothing and puts the card back. After the five seconds the block reads "Sending to …", then "Sent to {agent}"; a refused answer puts the card back with the reason. Leaving the chat sends what is held at once, and so does hiding the page. Closing the page while an answer is still held asks first; an answer the close cuts off is not sent, and its card stands open again next time. A sent answer that never reached the agent takes its block away once the asking thread is read again and shows the card open (at once for the Co-Founder's own questions; for another agent's, when its thread is next read), and the thread draws the open card again. The blocks are kept while the page is open (no answer can be withdrawn once sent, and no read lists past answers yet); while a block stands, its question is not drawn a second time in the thread. The dock no longer shows a card as sent for a moment: the block says it.
- A decision too tall for its room in the Co-Founder's chat shows that it goes on (seen live, 20 Nov 2026: in a small window a question with three pictures was cut at the dock's height with nothing to say so, its field out of sight). The card fades out at its lower edge while more of it lies below, and the fade goes once it is scrolled to its end.
- Open decisions are gathered in the Co-Founder's chat (thread decisions step 2, first piece; Jonas, 18 Nov 2026: "the decision hub should be moved to the co-founder chat"; 20 Nov: "Yes, build it"). The question cards that wait for the member in every agent's thread stand right above the Co-Founder chat's message box, one at a time, each drawn as its thread draws it: the asking agent's figure beside the card (above its corner in a narrow window), and under it a tab for each open decision with the agent's figure and the first words of the question. A tab, or the left and right arrow keys on the tabs, flicks between them; their order never changes (the one that runs out first, then the oldest). An answer goes to the asking agent exactly as in its own thread (`qorpera_answer_interaction`, the member's own model account for that thread); the card says it was sent and the next one comes up. A card that stands there is not drawn a second time in the Co-Founder's own thread. Questions only for now: approvals and email drafts follow with a database read of their own. The head line of a waiting card names the agent in its own element (`<b>`), which the dock draws in the text colour beside a quiet "asks you".
- The Claude login card shows no 5-hour bar (the founder, 20 Nov 2026: "remove the 5H limit, they already have"). Anthropic's own usage answer for a Claude plan has no 5-hour window any more (`five_hour: null`, only weekly windows), yet the card drew "5 hours · Starts when you next use Claude · 100% left". A Claude reading's `five_hour` window is now passed over when the cloud's list is read, so readings stored before today draw only their weekly bars; a reading made only of one shows "Usage unavailable" until the next read, unless Claude Code said the plan refused: "Usage limit reached · resets in …" still shows then (the reading's `limitResetAt`), whatever limit it named, with a held reset on its own tile. A 5-hour reset Anthropic's screen still lists stays on the card in Anthropic's words; using one marks no bar as refilling. GPT's own 5-hour window is unchanged.
- Words typed on a question keep the decision in sight (thread decisions step 1; Jonas, 18 Nov 2026: "after giving feedback on a question it stops having a pending question widget, though we have not landed on anything yet"; 20 Nov: "Yes, build it"). A question answered with the member's own words, none of its options, is their note: while the agent works on it and has not come back, the thread shows the card as "{agent} is reworking this" with the question and the note, the agent's figure at work at its head. Once the agent has answered or asked again it is the exchange it was, and a question with pictures no longer shows its options greyed with none chosen. An address card's typed address is its answer, as before.
- The company's own views come first in the apps drawer, and a website opens live inside Qorpera with its numbers (company views step 4, slice V2; the lane's DESIGN-4.md; Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "Yes, platform draws it" and "Yes, keep the four"; needs migrations `20260930767000`, `20260930768000` and `20260930774000` live). One row above everything else in the drawer: each live website of the company (its globe and title; one Qorpera took down, by `qorpera_company_site_access`, is left out), then the apps at `apps/products`, `apps/money` and `apps/ads` where they are made, posted or not; a view not made yet shows nothing, and the member's own arrangement keeps the rest. A website opens at `#/c/<id>/site/<slug>`: the real site in one big window on a warm paper, opened with a member's pass from `qorpera_site_member_pass` (again on Reload and for each page picked): the window loads the site door `/site-door/`, a page of the app's own that holds the frame the site shows in; the view hands it the pass by `postMessage`, and it posts it (`pass`, and `path` for a page) to `https://<slug>.qorpera.com/__qorpera/member` into that frame, never in an address. The door is its own page because the site's host takes a pass only with the app's exact `Origin`, and the app sends no referrer, under which its own form would say `Origin: null`; only the door's rule sends its origin (`Referrer-Policy: strict-origin`), and its policy lets it frame only the sites' hosts, post only to that path and run only its own script; its address above it and a link out in a new tab. Beside it on a wide page, under it on a narrow one: the people on the site now (`qorpera/site-now`, asked every 15 s while the view is open), a quiet line with today's visits, one curve of the last 24 hours (`site-hours`) and today's three most seen pages (`site-pages` for the company's day) by readable names ("Front page", "Lampen"; two alike take their parent's name first), never a path, each moving the window to that page. A number that can't be read is a quiet dash; a site that can't be shown says why in plain words (hosts off, taken down, not live yet, too many passes at once), with the link out where it works. The frames are sandboxed without top navigation. The app's own policy is unchanged: it frames only itself, so no Company File page or interface app may go to a site's host; only the door's policy frames `https://*.qorpera.com`. A member's own program never takes an owed view's folder: New program names a dropped `Money.apk` `apps/money-2`, and a program kit at an owed folder is no owed view.
- An interface app may show the company's own numbers, door `money` (company views step 3, slice W1; the lane's DESIGN-3.md §E; Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "Yes, start step 3"; needs migration `20260930767000` live). A page asks them as questions of the reserved database `qorpera` with the calls it has: `qorpera.query("qorpera/money-now")`, `qorpera.watch(...)`, a kit's binding. The bridge sends them to `qorpera_member_company_numbers` as the member (`cloud/company-numbers.ts`, a plain cloud call, so also where the build names no member gateway), only for an app whose `reads` names `money` (`money-*`) or `site` (`sites`, `site-*`, the site's visits, answered from migration `20260930768000`, slice S1); `data` reads none of them and they need no `data`; another `qorpera/*` name is no question. A watch is asked again on its own clock while the tab is shown, `site-now` every 15 s, other site questions every minute, money every 5 minutes, and the page hears only an answer whose etag changed (`CompanyNumbers`, ids below zero beside the databases' watches). An app whose `reads` loses `data`, `money` or `site` ends the watches it may no longer ask, and keeps the others. RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §5.
- Every member who signs in with email gets a password (Jonas, 19 Nov 2026: "get every existing user to create a password next time they log in"). A signed-in member whose account has no password yet sees "Choose a password" the next time the app opens, before anything else and after "What should we call you?" if that is due too; it cannot be skipped. The screen is the sign-in page's own (the crown, the new-password field with Show, at least 8 characters); saving goes straight on into the app. A member who signs in only with Google is not asked. The account carries `password_set` in its own details once a password is made here, at sign-up, by a reset or in Settings; nothing else needs to change for it.
- A real Qorpera account (Jonas, 17 Nov 2026: "fix the signup/login flow. you currently just use your email, you don't really create an account"; on the design page: layout "C", then "Passwords + code"). Two doors on one centered page with the crown above the heading, looking down at the form and blinking, and the logo top left; no split page, no footer line. Create your account takes a name, an email and a password of at least 8 characters, then a 6-digit code from the email proves the address and signs the member in, already named. Welcome back signs in with the password, or "Email me a code instead" for an account that exists (a code never makes an account any more). A password that matches no account says so in plain words with links to reset it or create an account; an address that already has an account says so, with Sign in instead. Forgot password sends a code; the code and the new password go on one screen and sign the member in. The code is six boxes that take a paste and go on by themselves at the last digit; a new code can be asked for after a minute. Auth's own error words are never shown. A member without a name (made with a code before this) is asked "What should we call you?" once, before the app. Settings, General has a Password row to set or change one. A visit from qorpera.com with an idea opens on Create your account. Danish too. Needs the login service's settings first (codes of 6 digits, the new email templates in `auth-email/`, the password changed notice), so this rides with that change.
- The sign-in emails and the invitation email in one look (same request; `auth-email/README.md` has each template's subject): the logo over one white card, the code as six large tiles and in the subject, a last line saying why the email came. The link that worked only in the same browser is gone; the code works everywhere. New: reset password and password changed. - Open and the close button of an agent's computer are readable while the computer starts at night (seen on renders, 19 Nov 2026): on the lit start scene they kept the dark ink meant for a light picture, on the night's dark ground. - Settings reads ChatGPT and each Claude login one after another, never together (seen live on 18 Nov 2026: one Refresh began a Claude read and, 50 ms later, the ChatGPT refresh, which the cloud answered "Account refresh already in progress", HTTP 500 in the browser). (a login that is Not in use: "Usage updates once this login is in use and Claude works on a task."): an idle sign-in's key lapses after some hours and only a Claude task renews it, so until then a read finds nothing new.
