At a limit the row lifts onto its own tile; a reset only usable at a limit shows no button until then; while a reset runs the bar it refills springs back up; then "Reset used", "Couldn't reset your limits · Nothing was used" with Try again, or "Couldn't confirm the reset went through" without another try. - An interface app's module scripts import its own files (deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §2, 18 Nov 2026; founder: "Yes, fix it", after Klokkesten's 3D page never opened its model). `<script type="module">`, by `src` or inline, resolves static and `export … from` imports with relative paths at any depth, bare names the page's import map gives a file of the folder (exact, or by a prefix such as `three/addons/`), literal `import("./x.js")`, cycles, and JSON and CSS modules (`with { type: "json" }`, `"css"`), as an app's page and as a page opened by itself: the web reads the graph while it puts the page together (es-module-lexer 2.3.1, MIT, vendored) and writes an import map from `qorpera-module:/<path>` names to each module's blob, so each runs once and a stack names the file and line. `import.meta.url` is the module's place in the frame, so `fetch(new URL("./x", import.meta.url))` reads the file beside it. An import that is not a file of the folder fails alone with one `Qorpera:` line in the console. A module worker of up to 1,532 KiB starts from an address `qorpera.file` gives (from a base64 `data:` address, the frame's `worker-src` now `blob: data:`; a larger one says so in the console), its own imports not resolved; proven closed to the network in Chromium.
- An interface app opens first and reads each of its files when it uses it, with no cap on their number, size or kind (deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §2 and §4, 17 Nov 2026; Jonas: "also uncap the file-sizes"). The page comes with the files its first paint needs as blobs (scripts, stylesheets and what they name, hidden pictures, and the first 24 pictures, videos and sounds of up to 2 MiB within 16 MiB); the rest are read through the frame's port when their element comes near the screen or is played. A script reads any file of its app by `fetch` or `XMLHttpRequest` of a relative path, `qorpera.file(path)` and `qorpera.bytes(path)`. The frame's policy adds only `blob:` and `data:` sources (scripts, fonts, fetch, workers), proven closed to the network in Chromium. "Opening…" shows how far a large app is. A page opened from the company folder by itself loads the files of its own folder and below the same way, and nothing else.
- A program app's page (RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §3f, 18 Nov 2026; Jonas: "Yes, build step 1"): an app of the kit `program` is drawn natively, not in a frame: its name, "Version 0.1 · <day>", its own pictures in a phone and a window (or one tile with the file's name and size), one Download for the member's own computer or phone (read from the browser; "Install" on an Android phone), the five kinds as chips, and the earlier versions in its "…" menu. A download goes through Storage's path, under the file's own name, and only while the file is the one the version was kept with. The page stays as it is while the folder changes under it. Goes live before the runtime bundle that carries the kit: an older web hides a program app.
- A member adds a program of their own (RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §3f, 18 Nov 2026; founder: "then build step 2, add your own"; needs the object gateway's member program route and the member company gateway function's allow-list first). The apps drawer ends with a dashed "New program" tile for members who write; its page is one drop area ("Drop a program here", "or choose a file"; "Choose a program" on a phone) and the Co-Founder's word in the chat's bubble. The kind comes from the file's name, or a question over the five kinds; the name from the file's (`Lagerliste-1.0-setup.exe` is Lagerliste), versions are 1, 2 and on. The tile fills while the file goes up ("Putting … in place… 40%", Stop), refusals are plain lines with Try again where it helps, and the program's page opens after with "Version 1 · added by you". A program a member made has Add a version in its menu and takes a drop. A new program claims its own folder before any byte goes up (the next slug when one is there); once the last change set is sent, Stop goes and it settles, and a stop before that deletes what was placed; the company folder's own refusal stops at once in its words, and a name the folder does not take says so. A change set or a file whose answer was lost is sent again with the same key before Try again or any delete, so nothing listed is deleted or listed twice; the newest version's very files dropped again are refused ("This is the version that is already here."), and an older version's are listed as a new one. Danish too. Live on merge: hold it until the gateway and the function are live.
- A company can start with no name, and picks its own email address (Jonas, 17 Nov 2026: "go with your picks, build it"; design page "Company mail addresses", round 2; needs the cloud's migration `20260930796000` and the runtime's address card). An empty name box opens the company with no name: the idea is no longer made into a name, and the cloud stores "No name yet". Wherever the name shows (the hub, the sidebar, menus, Settings, the table, Meet, Friends) such a company reads "No name yet" in muted italic, with a dashed "?" where its figure would stand until it has a logo, and sentences that carry the name say "the company" ("Start the company"); deleting it by typing its name takes "No name yet". A question card whose input is a mail address is the address card: `hello.`, the company's part and the domain in one field, prefilled with the agent's first suggestion and its others as pills, checked as it is typed through `qorpera_company_mail_handle_status` (Free, Already yours, That one belongs to another company, Use a–z, 0–9 and hyphens), and Use this address makes `hello.<handle>` as the member and answers the card with the address; a member who is not a founder or an admin sees it without a way to choose. Add address in the Mailbox asks for the first part alone once the company has a handle (`qorpera_company_mail_handles`), and shows `.<handle>@<domain>` after it; with none yet it asks for the company's part too, with the same check, and the first address claims it; a company with no name and no handle sees "Your email address waits for a name" instead.
- A run ChatGPT turned away for too many requests at once reads as that, not as a login to check (founder, 17 Nov 2026: "overnight I had a ton of failed runs, despite my GPT login still having usage"; the cloud's migration `20260930801000`, contract 17's 2026-11-17 amendment). A waiting run (`paused_for: "rate_limited"` in the agents' activity) reads "ChatGPT is busy with too many requests at once. This work waits a few minutes and then tries again by itself. The login is fine." with no other login to continue on; a failed one (`rate_limited` in the recovery read) reads "ChatGPT turned this run away because too many requests came at once. The login is fine." for every reader and offers Try again where the cloud allows it. A member's own message says it plainly (founder, 17 Nov 2026: a retry "or a visible 'didn't go through'"): to the member who sent it, a failed `session_turn` or `room_delivery` reads "Your message didn't go through: ChatGPT got too many requests at once. The login is fine.", with Try again where the cloud allows it (rooms included); other readers, and an agent's own runs, read the run's words (the recovery read's `source_kind` and `requested_by_user_id` decide). Danish too. An older cloud says neither, so the cards read as before: this goes live before the migration.
- The table where friends start a company together has the new-company screen's look (Jonas, 17 Nov 2026: "multiplayer company setup uses the old layout"). The name is the same centred box with no "Company name" label ("Name your company" when empty), the idea is the same large composer ("Describe your idea"), and the Co-Founder's crown stands large in the bottom-left corner looking up at it instead of peeking over the box; the founders' faces still look over its edge, and the seats, Ready and Start stay under it. Start makes the crown cheer, and every founder then sees the same starting scene a founder alone sees (the name, the idea, the founders' faces with "{names} own it now.", "Your Co-Founder is getting ready") until the company opens. The started page is left for a setup that failed ("could not be set up") and for a company whose Co-Founder waits on a login or did not start ("has started", with why); its "is starting" heading and "Preparing" spinner are gone, as no table reaches them now. The crown also stays through the table's first read, so "Invite friends" carries the same crown from the new-company box to the table. Danish too.
- Writing a long idea no longer throws the page back up (Jonas, 17 Nov 2026: "when you've written enough that you have to scroll, it will force teleport you up, so you can't see the bottom"). A text box that grows with its words measured itself by shrinking for a moment, which pulled the page's scroll up to fit; on the founding table that happened on every letter and on every read of the table. It now puts every scrolled box around it back where it was (`growToText`, `ui/dom.ts`), on the founding table and the new-company box.
- The empty name box on the new-company screen always reads "Name your company" (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: "make it always say "Name your company""); it no longer shows the name the idea would give. Left empty, the company starts with no name yet (the entry above).
- Lighter motion while agents work (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: "The load of running qorpera seems to be crazyyyy. I think its the animations"). A figure draws at most about 60 frames a second, the speed its motion is made at, on any screen of 60 Hz or faster: on a 120-165 Hz screen it drew on every refresh, its effects layer included (measured on one open conversation at 165 Hz: about 1,200-2,600 canvas redraws a second before, about 460-500 after; a 60 Hz screen is unchanged). The ink wave over "Working" and "Writing" now fades a crest copy of each letter in and out instead of changing the letter's colour and outline, so it runs on the graphics chip without repainting or laying out the text (about 10-37 layouts a second down to 2-4); still frames of it are pixel-identical at rest and alike mid-wave.
- The new-company box says what to write in the fields themselves (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: "let's remove the "Company name" and rename "Named after your idea" to "Name your company" Then instead of "The Idea" it should say "describe your idea" as the text were the "A webshop with ceramics" currently is."). No "Company name" label over the name box (it keeps the name for screen readers): empty, it reads "Name your company". No "The idea" label in the composer: its placeholder is "Describe your idea", and the example ideas no longer write themselves into it. Danish too.
- Extra high on GPT in the Model menu (founder, 16 Nov 2026: "Extra high only"; the cloud's migration `20260930788000`, contract 17's second 2026-11-16 amendment). The cloud's answer now names `efforts`: for each model it offers, the efforts this conversation may pick with it. The effort slider shows those stops: four on GPT (Low, Medium, High, Extra high) where the cloud names Extra high, else three as before; Claude keeps five. A switch from Claude to GPT steps an effort GPT lacks down to the highest it has (Max to Extra high where GPT has it, else High). Safe before the cloud change: a cloud without `efforts`, or whose bundle does not declare Extra high, names GPT low to high, and the menu offers no more.
- The new-company box says what to write in the fields themselves (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: "let's remove the "Company name" and rename "Named after your idea" to "Name your company" Then instead of "The Idea" it should say "describe your idea" as the text were the "A webshop with ceramics" currently is."). No "Company name" label over the name box (it keeps the name for screen readers): empty, it reads "Name your company". No "The idea" label in the composer: its placeholder is "Describe your idea", and the example ideas no longer write themselves into it. Danish too.
- Owners and admins can rename the company again, from the pencil beside its name in Settings → Company → Brand (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: "dont think you can really change the name of your company currently. could we fix that"; renaming was deferred on 9 Nov). Saving rewrites the first line of `QorperaOS/vision/vision.md`, re-reading it when it changed in between, and the cloud copies the name to the company within seconds, as it already did for a vision an agent edits; mail sends under it from then on. The new name shows at once in Settings, the sidebar and the company list, which reads the companies again until the cloud has it. A save that fails keeps the field open with what was typed.
- One click moves all of a member's chats that wait on a used-up login to another of their own logins, in a cleaner card (migration `20260930785000`; Jonas, 16 Nov 2026: clicking "Use …" in each chat one by one, then "make it a much cleaner widget"). The card under a chat whose own login is at its usage limit is one row: a small ring, "Out of usage until <time>" (the reset from that login's usage window; "Out of usage" with none known) over "Keep going with <account>", and the buttons on the right: "All <n> chats" and "Just this one" while at least one other open chat of the member's waits on the same login (n counts this chat too; `qorpera_count_member_sessions_on_account`, asked once for each login the chat is stuck on, not on every account read), else "Continue". The account offered is the first of the member's other logins, in the order the buttons used before; the others follow under the row as "Or use <account>, <account>", each a single pick for this chat. A login that cannot be used for another reason gets the same row with its reason as the title; with no other login, the line under the title says a message waits for the reset. On a phone (520 px and narrower) the buttons go under the words at full width and the words wrap, so an email is never cut. The old sentences ("This conversation's model account has reached its usage limit…", "Or use another of your logins now.") are gone; the retry and "Choose a login to continue" states stay as they were. One click, no confirm: `qorpera_choose_member_sessions_account` makes the member's own pick for each of those chats with the single pick's own step (`qorpera_take_member_session_account`, which `qorpera_choose_member_session_account` now uses too, reading the member's logins without working out their room), so its rules hold, and a turn waiting at the old login's limit goes on with the new login. The card then becomes the ring with a tick and "<n> chats moved to <account>". A chat whose waiting work cannot go with it (onto or off a ChatGPT plan, or from a login that works while the member is away onto one that does not) stays on its login and is not counted; when that is this chat, the card says so in place ("<n> other chats moved to <account>" over "This chat stays on its login: the work waiting in it can't move to <account>.") and offers no buttons. Nothing moves without that click, only between the member's own logins, never another member's chats, closed chats or agents' routine runs. Rides the next release with its migration.
- Starting a company is one scene from the box to the company (Jonas, 16 Nov 2026, on the design canvas "New company screen": A, then "the composer in A1 is better, but the name should not be inside the composer... it should be a centered box above it", then "what if we made the crown A LOTTTT bigger, but put him in the bottom left corner, looking up at the composer instead? then we center the composers more and make them bigger too?", "make the crown even bigger, remove the "Named after your idea...." and the "what do you want to build?"", "on send/startup, the crown should do the celebration animation ... let's make a smooth transition", and "Yes, build both"). The page is the company name as a centred box of its own (left empty, the company starts with no name yet) over a larger composer whose field is labelled "The idea"; the headline, the subtitle and the row of kinds of company are gone (a kind picked on qorpera.com still puts its example in an empty box). The Co-Founder's crown stands large in the bottom-left corner as the working figure, its eyes turned up toward the composer (a new `gaze` option for a figure, `ui/figure-motion.ts`), 220 px on a phone. It is one figure on the page (`app/views/start-crown.ts`) from the box until it lands in the company: the arrow makes it cheer while the name and the composer fold away; the company's page then shows the name large, the idea under it, a thin bar and "Your Co-Founder is getting ready" while the crown works, and keeps that scene until the company opens (no "is starting" page for a founder starting alone); then the rail slides in and the crown shrinks into the Co-Founder in it. A company that is ready while the way into it does not come (the company list could not be read) shows "Open {name}" on the same scene after 3 s. The ChatGPT step no longer flashes for a member who has an account login: a new company gets that login linked a few seconds after it is made (2 s on the live database, 16 Nov 2026), so the page waits up to 8 s for it, reading every 1.5 s, before it asks, and asks on the same scene. Danish too.
- Ask from the Data page's list, and kept questions that take values (slice 10 part g; deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-COMPANY-DATABASES.md §5a, "Part g as built"): a line under the list sends the member's question to the Co-Founder as the member's own message, through the same road as an app's note (app/tell-agent.ts), and opens that conversation, where the answer comes as the Co-Founder's message. The message opens with the list it was asked on, `> page:data#<database>.<table> · <about>` (`#<database>:<question>` for a kept question), which the conversation draws as the Data page's card (its drawers, "Data", the database and the list in words) and which opens that list; nothing technical shows. A thread whose draft already answers another page's note keeps the question in the line and says so. A database whose questions name `:viewer` has no Ask: the Co-Founder's thread is the company's. A kept question that takes values is a tab now too: a field a value above the list (`:viewer` stays Qorpera's), and the list reads once each is filled in; a question whose name the gateway would refuse is no tab. A kept question's column of days shows them as the table's days do, and the tabs fade at whichever edge they run past.
- The Data page (slice 10 part e; deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-COMPANY-DATABASES.md §5a; migration `20260930782000`; Jonas, 15 Nov 2026: "a database fits best as a list", and "Yes, both" to members seeing and changing whole tables): a built-in page beside Mailbox and Bank, shown in the apps row with a chest of drawers while `describe` lists a database the member may read. One table at a time as a list, its tables and its kept questions without parameters as tabs, column heads that sort through the gateway (`order`, `desc`), pages that follow by key (`next`/`after`), and the open table watched live. A row opens with its fields. Where the member may write the folder, a table with a key takes a new row and changed fields as the member makes them, with no Save button; a row someone else changed meanwhile shows as it is now, in plain words; only a removal asks first, on the bar above the page. Views and tables without a key are read only and say so.
- What changed on the Data page (slice 10 part f; deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-COMPANY-DATABASES.md §5a, "Who changed what"; the design canvas "What changed, and who did it"): a "What changed" button beside a database's tabs, for a member who gets its list. It draws the last two weeks as the rows changed each day by the member's agents, the member and the company's other members, and under it says each change of the chosen day (the latest day with changes at first) as one plain sentence with who and when: "Kaya set Ready to Yes on row 1 of Repairs", "You added Raleigh Tourist to Repairs". Who is named from the members and agents the page already holds. Earlier pages of the record are read as a chosen day needs them. Danish too.
- The Live mark of an agent's computer stands in the top-left corner of the small window (seen on the live app after v248, 15 Nov 2026): at the bottom-left it lay over the date in the computer's own dock. In the large view it stays in the title, as before.
- An agent's computer that is awake only because a member woke it pauses its window sooner (migration `20260930759000`; Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "if a person has an idle tab in qorpera open, we should be able to close the machines when the user clearly isn't actually online", and "let's go with 10 minutes"). Such a computer sleeps 10 minutes after the member's last sign, so its window stops looking and asks "Paused to save power. Still watching?" after as long without a touch on the page (the seconds its list names, `memberSleepSeconds`; never more than the 30 minutes every other computer keeps). For it the member's word that they watch follows their touch within a minute (every 5 minutes otherwise), and is also said while the live picture shows, on one look that is not drawn, so a member who is there is never taken for gone. Keep watching is now the member's ask for the computer, as a click on Computer is: one that fell asleep under the paused window is woken by that press, not by a second one. A computer an agent works on, and one from a database before the migration, behaves as before.
- A member opens one of the company's apps on an agent's computer (migration `20260930758000`): Open, beside the title in the large view and with the hover controls of the small window, lists the company's apps by name; choosing one is the ask (asleep: woken, its screen turned on and the app opened; off: turned on with it; on: opened at once; from the small window the large view comes at the first picture). Refusals in plain words, in English and Danish: not in the company folder any more; this computer cannot see the company folder yet; the computer could not open it (with Try again); someone else's private typing as a click's. Opening a file of the folder is left out: the web has no picker of the company folder to reuse (Storage's browser has no choose mode).
- A working figure no longer juggles stars (Jonas, 15 Nov 2026: "the avatars which animations weren't good enough should just only use the eyemovement + thread animation that we took from grok bot. let's just keep using it. the avatars with avatar specific animations should have those AND the thread animation, just switch between them more"; on the page that showed it: "Yes, build it"). Every working figure stays still while a thread passes round it, behind and in front, its eyes following the tip, with slow glances and blinks between passes; each pass takes its own tilt, height and direction, and every third swings up over its head. Planet, crown, mushroom and puzzle have the thread alone. Acorn (a seedling grows out of its stalk), flame (a flare one time, sparks the next), rocket (lifts, tilts to the right and flies in place on a flame from below, the one effect that moves the figure), heart (the heartbeat line), star (a shooting star orbits it), cloud (a short shower), bolt (static from its edges, then a strike to the ground) and moon (stars twinkle round it) take turns between the thread and their own effect, with 2 s of rest between (`ui/figure-work.ts`; drawn on two `.figure-air` canvases behind and in front of the figure). A figure whose work also waits on the member still becomes its "?" or "!" between spells, now at the end of a whole loop, when the figure is at rest, and at the needs-you act's pace. Reduced motion: the figure rests with its eyes open.
- A computer the member opens comes into view (found on the live app, 15 Nov 2026): Computer on an agent's card, or Open computer on a take-over card, brought the window in at the thread's end, under the composer, and the thread stayed where it was. The thread now goes to the window: at the thread's end it stays at its end while the window grows into its place; a window that stands further up, where the agent left it, comes into view between the thread's top and the foot. The window takes the keyboard without the browser's own scroll, which knew nothing of the foot and left the thread just short of its end, where it no longer followed. A window that shows by itself still never moves a thread the member has scrolled.
- An agent's computer window no longer lets a member pick among the agent's machines (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, on the switcher in its corner: "don't get why I can pick between machines - not even agents.. and they mostly dont have a computer open. remove I say"). The switcher, its menu and its words are gone from the row and from the large view. The window shows one computer and chooses it itself: the first with a screen as the list names them (a running one before the others, the one whose screen the agent used last before the rest), so it follows the agent; in the large view it stays on the computer the member is at while that one runs, and follows the list again from the row. `ComputerPanel.choose` keeps the old machine change for the tests of the hold machinery alone, and goes with it.
- The web no longer shows or reads an agent's wall (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "rather drop the wall - it's the wrong medium for activity"; asked whether the one line under each agent's name should stay when the wall goes: "Let it go too"). The card above an agent's composer ("N waits on you", its items and their Answer) is gone, with its reader of `QorperaOS/agents/<handle>/wall.md` (`app/agent-wall.ts`, `ui/agent-wall-poster.ts`, `styles/agent-wall.css`, the thread's `agentWall` hooks and the inline Markdown it alone used); a `wall.md` left in an agent's folder is never asked for, and the state folders are no longer read for it when a company opens. The coding sessions' pill no longer shares a line with the wall: it stands on its own line right above the composer. In the rail, an idle agent's line no longer says "Waits on you: …", the wall's Now line or what it last got done: between runs it reads, first that holds, that its run did not finish, that it rests until a time, its next planned run (however far off: the 12-hour rule only ranked it against the wall's last-done line), or when it was hired. A working agent, and one that needs the member ("Needs you", read from question and approval cards), keep their own line. What stays: a message that opens with a quoted line (`> question`, an empty line, the member's words), as members answered the wall's questions since 7 Nov 2026, still shows the question above the member's words in its bubble; notes from an interface app (`> app:<name>#<id> · <about>`) are sent, wait and are drawn as before. Web only; the agents' side drops its wall tool in a runtime release of its own.
- An interface app may read the company's reading of itself: `qorpera.reading()`, for a page whose manifest's `reads` names `reading` (Jonas, 15 Nov 2026, on the company's page: "it should not be the platform that draws it, rather an agent should maintain it. users should also get a "retire interface" config when rightclicking an interface to stop maintaining it, and hide it. then adding layers and complexity makes sense, as it let's the user dive into what's being made"; asked how the agent should make it: "Use a map kit"; RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §5). The web reads `QorperaOS/vision/reading.md` as the member (`app/company-reading.ts`; its bytes only when it changed; a file that does not read keeps the last reading that did) with the company's agents and its standing (`qorpera_read_company_standing`, `cloud/company-standing.ts`; null from a backend without it, asked no more), and hands the page plain names, never a path, a handle, a user id or an agent id: the reading's things with the agents at them (name, figure, working; one the company does not have, or whose role ended, left out) and the members they wait for (`me` for the member looking), what changed, the team with the Co-Founder first, and whether the company stands still and why (`you` when the login that holds it is the member looking's, `member` another member's name, neither when nobody can be named). An ended agent of a change line is found by its own `agent.md`, else by the platform's folder name for its name (`companyStateName`). The standing is read every 30 s while such a page is shown and the tab is, and when the tab comes back; a change of it reaches the page as a change notice. The `map` kit draws it.
- Over an agent's computer the member keeps their own pointer (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "let's actually hide the agent cursor when inside of the agents computer"). While the member took over, the live picture swapped their pointer for the desktop's own arrow, a small low-resolution picture that looked blurred on a sharp screen. The desktop's pointer shape is no longer shown or read (`ui/machine-screen-view.ts`); its messages still come and are answered at once. The agent's arrow inside the picture itself is TigerVNC's: the live picture never moves the pointer, so TigerVNC drew the arrow into its pixels. The machine image's X server now starts with `-nocursor` (`deploy/machine/desktop-session`), which hides it once machines run an image built from this change; until then that arrow still shows.
- An agent's computer that came into its thread by itself stays there after the agent stops, touched or not, as a still where it stood (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, asked whether such a window should stay too: "Yes, keep it"). Only the member closes it. The rule that took an untouched window away a minute after the agent stopped is gone (`computer-by-itself.ts` keeps only once-per-stretch and the member's close), and with it the window's claim and close-by-rule (`ComputerPanel.claim`, `closableByRule`, `closeByRule`) and the company's feed of it in `application.ts`.
- A new group in the apps drawer starts folded (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, on a group just made: "it should also start out collapsed - currently it would be expanded on creation"). Letting one app go over another makes one tile where the app under it stood, and the two apps shrink into it as a folding group's apps do; a press opens it. Before, the group opened at once, its two apps under it, and was kept as open on the device. A tile on its way out now stays in the grid until its way out ends whatever is drawn meanwhile, never taken out and put back: a draw that changed no tile (the answer to the write that made the group, a company update) took it out mid-fold.
- What's new can show a ready version before it goes live, and Sites and What's new say when Qorpera took a site down
  (own code storage step 5.1, part E1b and the cross-check's fixes; migration `20260930756000`;
  `lanes/own-code-storage/STEP5-BUILD.md`). While the releases read says `preview` (owners and admins, only while site
  hosts are on), the ready block shows "Look first" before "Put it live": the press opens a blank tab within the click
  with its way back to the app cut at once, asks `qorpera_open_site_preview` for a one-time link, and sends the tab
  there; the link must be exactly `https://<the site's slug>--next.qorpera.com/.qorpera/enter?t=<ticket>` and is used
  once and kept nowhere. Every other answer closes the tab and says why in its own words (a page that moved is read
  again; a site taken down; no answer; any other failure; not an owner or admin); `off` takes the button away. Put it
  live and Go back read the new `refused` answer ("It did not go live: Qorpera took this site down. Nothing changed.").
  Sites reads `qorpera_company_site_access` beside the list, leniently: a site Qorpera took down shows "Taken down" and
  one sentence with the reason in place of its link and password ("Qorpera took this site down: <reason>. If you think
  this is wrong, tell us with Feedback in your menu.", the account menu's item by its own label; no mail address), and
  no menu (the lead's call, 13 Nov 2026: a takedown sticks until Qorpera restores it, and the kernel refuses Take
  offline meanwhile; a menu still open, or a question still asked, when the read says so sends nothing). A project's site
  taken down before it ever went live, which no list has, gets the same row from the access read's names (the review of
  #1774, F4: the list's closed row shape is left as it is, so an open tab keeps reading it). What's new reads the same
  access beside its page and offers no "Open the site" for a site taken down, saying so instead, for a site never live
  too, and none of what the kernel refuses then: no Look first, no Put it live, no Go back, only Not yet (review round
  3); it takes the names from the access read, and reads the list only for an entry that names none. The Danish says "hjemmesiden" as the rest of Sites does. A backend without these (today's) draws both
  pages exactly as before.
- An agent's computer is a row of its thread, beside the agent (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "when an agents computer is open, instead of having it sit on the outside of the thread in the top right, how about a bigger window next to the working agent inside of the thread?"; he picked "A with C" of the sketch). The window takes the thread's width (up to 980 px wide, 16:10) next to the agent's figure, with a line under it ("Nova is using its computer"). While the agent uses its computer the row is the thread's live end, in the working figure's place, and new messages come in above it; once it stops, the row stays where it stood and what comes after goes under it, and the window says the computer is asleep once it sleeps. It is no longer dragged or resized (`ui/computer-drag.ts` is gone; `ui/computer-place.ts` keeps the press's focus mark and fixes the large view over the thread it stands in), and the large view still covers the thread, all but its header.
- An agent's figure no longer shows a colour it does not have while the page loads (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, after a reload: "a lot of the agents load in their old colors for a brief moment before they load the actual profile they have"). Until its drawn figure was ready, each figure showed its still artwork, which is the figure's original look: neither the agent's saved colour nor the figure's own shade today (the planet's still is teal, its shade sky blue; the heart's orange, its shade red). Now the figure's place stays empty until the figure is drawn in its own colour, and it fades in (`ui/figure-motion.ts`). A figure whose artwork in that colour and motion are already loaded (a sidebar row built anew, another view of the agent) is drawn at once, before the browser shows it, where it showed the still for a frame before. The still artwork is shown only when the figure cannot be drawn (its motion failed to load). No migration, no gateway or runtime change.
- Opening a company no longer logs a server error while a check of the member's model account is still running (found by the desktop computers lead, 14 Nov 2026: `POST …/rpc/qorpera_begin_native_account_refresh` answered 500, code 55000 "Account refresh already in progress", at each company open while an attempt an earlier page had left behind was pending). The refresh reads first now (`qorpera_get_native_account_refresh`): a pending attempt is watched with the read, backed off as before, and admission is asked only when none is pending. An attempt that had completed before the page looked is not joined: only admission knows whether its result is still fresh. Admission can still answer busy when two pages ask in the same moment; it is then asked again only after a pause, as before, and an attempt seen to complete meanwhile is joined. One more light read per refresh. (`cloud/native-subscription.ts`.)
- Who answers an address also says how it answers, and the Addresses page is calmer (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, on the Addresses' "Choose who answers" menu: "when selecting who to answer, you should also be able to set instructions for how it answers"; then, on the first look: "It's a lot of text. I would rather the instructions hiding into the selected agent. I would also move the new mailbox up under addresses, and add a pill next to it for the digital post to remove some chaos. then let's get rid of the counter … and 'Active · Last email …'"). The instructions live in the agent's chip: under the agents its menu ticks, each agent who answers the address has a row, "Tell Ada how to answer" or, once there are words, "How Ada answers", which opens a panel beside the menu with the words in a field that grows with them, and Save (⌘/Ctrl-Enter) or Cancel. Nothing of them stands on the page. The Add form's chip has the same row; its words go into the new routine as the address is made, and are kept for the address when giving it to the agent fails, so the next agent given it has them. The words live in the agent's routine for the address under a heading of their own, `## How to answer`, the same in every language. The panel reads the routine's file as it opens and Save changes that file, instructions only (how it runs, its reply and effort stay), on the member's own model account as the routines' editor does: a change made to it meanwhile is found, not written over, and the member's words wait for a second Save; a save whose answer was lost goes again as it was. Where another member's own account pays for the routine now (`routines.payer`), the panel says saving moves it to theirs. Emptied, the words leave the routine. Words typed and not saved are kept when the menu closes without Save (the pointer on another row, the window left), and the panel opens on them again until Save or Cancel; where someone changed the routine's words since they were typed, the panel says so before a Save puts them in their place. Only an owner or admin sees and changes them, as they change who answers. Add mailbox now comes first, under the page's name, with a Digital Post pill beside it that opens Digital Post's card under them (it no longer stands open at the foot). An address's line says no more than its address, its purpose and who answers it: the count of the last 7 days and the state line ("Active · Last email …") are gone, and a paused address says "Paused". (`views/mailbox.ts`; `views/settings-hooks.ts` gets `state` and `top`; a menu panel's control marked `data-autofocus` takes the focus, `ui/context-menu.ts`.)
- An app's page asks the company's databases, live (RUNTIME-COMPANY-DATABASES.md §5): `qorpera.query(name, params)` answers a named question (`<database>/<question>`, at most 16 short parameters, never `viewer`) as `{columns, rows, more, asOf}`, each row an object by column name as `qorpera.table` gives rows (the later of two columns of one name stands; the etag stays in the web). `qorpera.watch(name, params, listener)` calls the listener with the first answer and again with each answer that differs (`qorpera-app-data` over the page's port, with the watch's id), and returns a function that stops it; a watch that can't start (no `data` in reads, a bad name, too many watches, a question that is not there or not readable, no member gateway) calls an optional fourth argument, `failed(error)`, once with the refusal's words, while what goes wrong after a watch started is not shown and the page keeps what it shows. An answer is handed to a page only when it fits 1 MiB, counted in UTF-8 bytes, in the shape the page gets (rows of named columns): a question's answer or a watch's first answer over it is refused with words that ask for fewer rows or columns, and no watch is kept; a later answer over it is not sent, the watch ends and gives its place back, and the page keeps the last answer it had. A question's parameters are kept as own properties, so ones named `__proto__` or `constructor` reach the gateway as given, and the same parameters in any order are one watched question. Both need `data` in the manifest's reads and cost one call of the minute; what a watch sends later costs nothing. A page holds at most 8 watches, the company's open apps 24 questions together. One hub per open company (`CompanyData`) carries every watch in one held call through the member gateway (`CloudMemberData`), shares one question watched twice, starts a new call when the set changes, waits 2 s, 5 s, then 10 s after a call that failed without telling any page, and stops while the tab is hidden, when the company is put away and, for a page's watches, when its frame goes or its manifest no longer reads `data`. A build that names no member gateway asks no database. A manifest may name `data` in its reads since #1744 (`APP_READS`), and the kit pages' live values (#1744, #1778) call `qorpera.watch`; a page whose manifest does not read `data` is refused with the words that say what to add.
- The large view shows the desktop live (migration `20260930745000`, POINTER-BRIEF.md step 2): with a stream address in the build's configuration (`machineStreamUrl`, `wss://screens.qorpera.com`, in the page's connect-src), a member who may act gets the desktop's changed areas as they change over one WebSocket, on the pointer pass of the hold or sharing the picture's input is for, instead of a picture every 450 ms. The ticket is bought on the pointer route; the stream only shows (the page never sends on it); its messages are drawn on a canvas of its own, strictly in the order they came (the size, the changed areas at their places, the desktop's pointer shape as the picture's own cursor while it takes input), while the window's own pictures keep their canvas: one of the two shows at a time, so neither can ever overwrite the other, and the live one shows from its first pixels until the stream ends. While it shows, the looks pause and the window renews the pass; it ends at once with its hold or sharing (Back, Type privately, Done, a switch, a hidden tab, a pause, a close), and the looks come back with a whole picture. A failed stream is tried again after 2 then 4 s; three failures in a row turn it off for 10 minutes; a refusal or the desktop's end leaves the window to its looks, which say why; a way without the route (an executor before it) keeps the looks quietly; a node that watches as many desktops as it may (`STREAM_BUSY`) leaves the looks and the sharing as they were and is asked again 30 s later. Watchers and the small window keep their looks. An area or a pointer shape still being decoded when a stream ends draws nothing, so a picture of the stream never lands over a newer look. From the v245 release review: a look of the window's own still decoding when the live picture's last frame is handed back no longer lands over it or calls the older screen live (the hand-back advances the look turn; F2); and only pixels drawn end a run of stream failures, so streams that send their size and then fail, again and again, reach the three-failure cutoff (F3).
- A question that waits for the member is calmer (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026, on a question with a picture: "its so chaotic and ugly"). Its frame is the cards' own quiet line on a soft shadow, not the accent outline and its breathing ring ("the border is red and ugly"); the agent's figure and "<Agent> asks you" still say it waits. An app is shown once ("the interface reference is there twice"): when the question's words link an app an option previews, that option is a pill like the others and the app's cover stands on the words' own app card, over its emblem and title; no second card under the options. Only an option with a picture of its own is a box ("the selections are unfitted"): the options without one are pills under the boxes, where they were boxes as tall as their words beside a picture, and a lone picture box keeps to half the card. A pill's words wrap inside the card, so a long answer never runs past it at phone width (review r1 F1; every question's pills, which kept to one line before). `refreshAppPreviews` also brings a linked app's cover onto its card once the company's apps are read. Answered questions, the questions dock and a member who cannot answer look as before.
- A line in a thread that says an agent and a coworker talked ("Note from Elektronik", "Messaged Nova", "Talked with Helper and Nova") shows no time on a hover (Jonas, 14 Nov 2026: "get rid of the hover timestamp for these sorts status/activity of messages"). Its coworker chips no longer carry `data-sent-at`, so `ui/sent-time-hover.ts` passes them by; a message's own time on a hover is as it was. A screen reader still hears the time as the chip's description.
- A web page a link in an app's page leads to opens only from Qorpera's bar, once the member has seen its whole address and pressed Open (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, asked whether a page should be able to carry what a member typed out through a link: "Show the address first"). The link is a request of its own on the bar above the page (`app/views/app-ask-bar.ts`, kind `link`): the title says the app wants to open a web page; the site stands first, a size up, the host as the browser will reach it (punycode stays punycode), its registered part in the bar's ink and any labels before it quieter, and a port when there is one; then the address in full, path, query and fragment, in the house monospace, wrapping, selectable, and scrolled inside its own line past six lines. Open wakes after a second, takes only a press begun after that, and is never where the focus lands; Not now and Escape open nothing; one request at a time; three Not now in a row quiet the app until it is reloaded. The tab opens in the member's press on Open, with its way back cut and no referrer, so the browser lets it open (should one still be stopped, Qorpera's offered line above the frame stays the way to it). It costs a request's five calls of the app's budget. A call that does not fit in what is left of the minute is refused whole and spends nothing, as the contract says: a note, an answer or a row change at 116 calls was let through past 120, and a web link refused there kept one call. An address with a name or password in it is still refused before any bar, and `app:`, `folder:` and `#…` links are as they were. A page of the Company File beside a chat still opens no links, and Storage's own preview of a page is as before.
- The Concepts viewer's side panel ends with its list (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, on the Reload and Storage buttons and the strip under them: "get rid of this at the bottom of the concepts sidepanel"), open, folded and at phone width. Reload and Show in Storage are in a concept's row menu, the rail's own (`ui/context-menu.ts`): a right-click, a long press on a touch screen (`pressToOpenMenu`, moved there from the rail; a press still held when the viewer, the rail or the apps drawer is put away now opens nothing), or Shift+F10 or the menu key on the focused row. Every row has Show in Storage; the open concept's row also has Reload, which also lets a concept that was told "Not now" three times ask again. The strip that said whose the open concept is and that Qorpera never asks for passwords there (security review r1 H2, #1539 round 1) is removed on his word; who made each concept still stands in its row, and nothing replaces the line, so nothing on screen tells a member that a sign-in a concept draws is not Qorpera's (RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §6 H2 says what else guards a member, and what does not). The line beside a chat is as it was, and an app's own page never had it.
- The sidebar toggle's glyph is drawn well (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "polish the sidepanel toggle icon"; it read thin and soft): on a 20 px grid of its own (`ui/dom.ts` `ICON_GRIDS`), a 1.5 px line with round joins and caps, its centres on quarter pixels so its edges fall on whole device pixels at 2x, an outline of 16 by 14 (the optical size of the rail's search and plus icons) with outer corners of 3.5, and the divider a third in from the panel's side. It is the same glyph wherever the sidebar is toggled (the Concepts viewer, and the phone's navigation button in a thread, a member chat, a new conversation, the company's top bar and the loading skeleton). In the Concepts viewer its button, its icon and the row they stand in are rounded to whole pixels (`round()`), and the panel's width too, and the panel's edge line is drawn inside it (an inset shadow, no border's width), so the glyph stands on the grid it was drawn for, open and folded: a 20 px icon, 13 px into a 46 px button, at the default scale. Its label and tooltip say what it does now, "Hide sidebar" or "Show sidebar", and `aria-expanded` whether the sidebar is open.
- The Concepts viewer's side panel is the company rail (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, on a company's concepts: "For the sidepanel in the concepts interface I would also polish it to match the style and width of the regular sidepanel"). It takes the width the member set for the expanded rail (`ui/rail-layout.ts` `expandedRailWidth`, with the rail's own default and cap): a compact rail's strip is no width for it, so the rail's default stands in then, and the panel's own fold stays its own and the rail's surface, top row, quiet section title, rows, hover, selection and text smoothing; its tokens are the rail's own (`styles/app.css`, `styles/dark.css`). The chrome around the concept is gone with it: no ring or inset on the open concept's row, no border on a cover, and no lines over the Reload row or the strip that says whose the concept is (Jonas: "In general I would try and minimize the borders/framing around the visual we are just trying to show"). Folded, the panel is the compact rail's strip.
- A question in the Concepts viewer's dock stands on the page of the concept it asks about and on no other (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: the question box "should only be on the concept page it's asking about"). One that points at no concept, or at one that is gone, shows on none and waits in its agent's thread. A card answered on one concept's page stays off another's for the moment it is held answered, also when its answer is taken after the member went there, and its departure never moves the card on screen there. On an app's own page nothing changes.
- The dock's question card is headed once (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "In the question box I would also simplify and remove 'Open the chat, QUESTION, Co-Founder · 02:45 PM, Waiting for you'"): the agent's figure and "<Agent> asks you", then the question, its body, its app links, its options and Skip. The way to the chat is gone, and the card's label row — its kind, the agent and the time, and the state chip — is kept for a screen reader only, as it already is in a thread.
- A question option's preview shows its visual (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, with a screenshot of his company's question: the option previews "do not load their visual"). A preview that names an app shows that app's `cover`, or the app's own emblem beside its own title, where it drew a grey file icon and the app's folder name; the app card under the options takes the app's title too. An option with no preview, or whose picture cannot be read, draws no preview box at all, where it drew an empty grey frame, and a box with no picture is no taller than its own words. A card drawn before the company's apps are read gets an app's cover, or its emblem and title, once they are, and again when the app changes: in a thread, on an app's page and in the Concepts viewer's dock, in place (`refreshAppPreviews`, run with the page's pass over app cards in `app/application.ts`), so a pick and an answer being typed stay.
- Sites reads a website at an address of its own (own code storage, step 5.1, part E1a: `lanes/own-code-storage/STEP5-BUILD.md`; Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "Use qorpera.com"). Once site hosts are turned on, the kernel answers a website's link in `qorpera.company-site/v1` as `https://<slug>.qorpera.com/`; the closed parse (`cloud/sites.ts`) took only `https://<origin>/<slug>/`, so every tab open then would have said the company's sites could not be read and lost Sites from the apps drawer. A website's link may now also be exactly its own host: https, the site's own name one label before `qorpera.com` by the one host rule SQL, site-serve and the web share (`^([a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*)(--next)?\.qorpera\.com$`, on the name the URL parser lower-cased; the name's length is its own rule), the path `/`, and no port, sign-in, query or fragment, not even an empty one. Another site's host and a `--next` host (the look at a version before it goes live) are refused: `shop--next` is always site shop's next host, never a name of its own, and `shop-next` is a plain name. The domain is pinned, not read from the answer: the row names none, and one taken from the link would let any host through. Today's answers read and draw exactly as before; the link pill, its copy and the open arrow work for both forms, with no new words. The regex's text `(--next)?\.qorpera\.com$` is the marker step 5.1's hosts-on gate looks for in the live web bundle. Web only; it goes live before the kernel can answer the new form.
- An action on a desktop waits for every pointer move of the page on that desktop that may still land, not only for its own hold's or sharing's (v242 release review r1 F1 of the member's pointer): a move on its way, or one whose answer never came until its pass has ended everywhere, is kept by machine in the company's ledger of desktops, so going Back and in again (a new sharing), typing privately, or closing the window and opening the desktop in a new one no longer lets a click go before an older move could still arrive after it.
- The agents' figures do nothing under the pointer, and a figure that needs the member or works plays a little slower (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, on the figures' acts as they went live with #1735: "please get rid of the quick jump animation when hovering over an agent. It shouldn't be doing anything. also think the needs your attention and the working animations are slightly too fast"). The small jump a rail figure made when the pointer came over its row is gone, with its moment (`perk`). Needs you (the jump and spin into "?" or "!", the symbol's hops and shakes, the look around) and Working (the juggling, its tricks, the ask in between) play at 0.85 of the pace they were written at, as does a writing figure's ask: `ui/figure-motion.ts` turns the figure's clock into the act's time (`PACE`), so the acts in `ui/figure-acts.ts` keep their own. The cheer, the writing dots and an idle figure's own moves are as they were.
- An agent at its desktop computer shows (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "when an agent IS using a desktop computer, the computers window pops up in it's threat for you to see. and perhaps an animation the avatar could do in the sidepanel to indicate it"; migration `20260930742000`). The company reads which agents use their computers now (`qorpera_list_company_screens_in_use`, `app/agents-at-computers.ts`): at its open, on a `machines` hint (the database sends one at each new stretch of an agent's use), on the page's return, on the heartbeat while hints are down, and at the end of an entry's window (the database's 90 s, carried as each entry's end), so nothing is read while nobody uses a computer. A working agent in that list is `computer`: the rail says "Using its computer" (Danish "Bruger sin computer") and its figure plays a new `AtComputer` state (`ui/at-computer-motion.ts`): lit from below by a screen's cool blue-white light that flickers through a few tints with now and then a short flash of a game's colour, its eyes darting in short moves with a rare blink, a small lean now and then, drawn only when something moves (about 15 repaints a second against Working's 60); reduced motion holds a still lit pose. The agent's thread shows the computer's small window by itself (`app/views/computer-by-itself.ts`): only in that agent's thread, without taking focus, not while the member has a large view open or types privately on a computer, once per stretch; a window the member closed stays closed through the stretch; one that came by itself and was not touched goes 60 s after the agent stopped. A cloud before the migration answers the read as missing: neither shows.
- The member's own pointer is the desktop's pointer in the large view (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "I also notice that im driving a different cursor that only moves when I click, making it so I cannot hover anything really, and feels much less smooth"; migration `20260930744000`). While the member's pointer is over the picture with no button pressed, the window sends where it is (`WebMachineScreens.pointer`): it begins a 10 s pointer pass (shared at the sharing's generation, or on the private hold's epoch and seat) and renews it, then sends the newest position only, one request at a time, at most 30 a second (a begin at most once a second, and after one that got no pass none until the member's next move); never while an action of the same sharing or hold waits (an action moves the pointer where it acts); leaving the picture stops it. In order with actions (review r1 of #1733): a move made while a click waits for its second, a wheel burst or text is gathered goes right after that action (F3); an action goes only once the move on its way was answered (F1), and after a move whose answer never came only once that move's pass has ended everywhere. The screen off, a restart or a sleeping machine pause the pointer 3 s, and it comes back with the desktop (F4). The desktop's pictures for this member then come without the drawn pointer, so there is one pointer, his own, and the window does not back off while it moves (a look every 450 ms, never sooner than the database's 400 ms: F5), so a hover shows within a look. A bridge or an executor without the pointer route (404) turns the pointer off for the page, quietly: today's way, the desktop's pointer drawn in the pictures. A refusal a new pass would repeat ends that sharing's pointer; `DESKTOP_HELD_SINCE` waits for a fresh list as an action's does. The canvas keeps the browser's own arrow; the desktop's cursor shape comes with step 2 (the held-open stream).
- A member can tell the agent that owns an interface app something about a thing in it, from the app itself (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "you can't act very much inside of the interfaces"). A page asks with `qorpera.tell({about, at, text})`: what the note is about (1 to 60 characters, made one line with no `@`, `«`, `»`, control or direction marks), optionally the thing's id in the app's data, and optionally a draft of the member's words (at most 280 characters). Any app may ask: `writes` has no word for it. The bar above the page that answers and row changes use shows it with one field more: "This app wants to send your note to <agent>", the lines About (the app's name · what the page said, as values) and Your note, a field of Qorpera's that starts with the page's draft and has the focus; "Send note" stays off while the field is empty and wakes after a second as every yes does, a key held since before the bar came in types nothing in the field, Enter there is a new line, and Escape on the bar is Not now (on every bar now). What goes is what the field holds, as the member's own message in the owner's shared thread, or the Co-Founder's when the app has no owner at work: a first line `> app:<name>#<at> · <about>`, then the member's words. The thread draws that line as the app's card, above the member's words, with what the note is about as its line; a press on it opens the app. A note into a thread with something drafted, or for a member with no model account there, waits in that thread's composer, and the bar says so with Open: its first line is what the draft answers (as a wall question is), shown above the field as the app's emblem and name and what the note is about, never as written, and its words go after what was drafted, so the send is one message under one card. Where the draft is saved and shared (the room's shared draft), that line is its text's first line, so a reload, another tab or another member's client has it with the words, and every check that a draft answers something reads the room's text too. A thread whose draft answers something already (a wall question, another app's note) or whose message is on its way takes no note: it stays on the bar with its words, a line says the thread has a message started, with Open, and it is kept for the app with the member's words until it went or they say not now, so the bar shows it again when they come back to the app (or its manifest changes under the page); while a send of it is on its way no bar shows it, and held again it comes back on the app's page on screen. An app has at most one note, from the moment the member's words exist until they went, wait in the thread or the member said not now: meanwhile the page's next note is refused ("A note from this app is on its way: ask again in a moment", or "An earlier note from this app is still waiting above the page: send it or say not now first") and the kept note is on the bar again where none shows it; a kept note whose send fails keeps its bar, with why and the member's words. Changes the room merges into such a draft reach the composer cut at the line, so a quote stays whole. The page is answered `{sent: true}` or `{sent: false, waiting: true}`, or told why not; a note costs five calls of the app's budget, and the same app's next note is refused for ten seconds after one went (counted from when it went, also when the member left the app meanwhile). `CompanyMail.tell` is lifted into `app/tell-agent.ts`, which the Mailbox's notes go through too. Two fixes reach the Mailbox's notes with it: they wait while the member is answering a question on that agent's wall (a send would have put the question in front of the note), and they are sent from the Mailbox at all. The app's input writer sends a message only while its thread is on screen, so a note from the Mailbox always failed with "Company access is unavailable." and waited in the thread as a failed send; a note on its way now counts the member's press as the thread on screen (`CompanyController.sendNote`), every other check of a send holding. Generate icon's ask in the apps drawer (`requestAppIcons`) failed the same way from any page but its agent's thread, and now goes the same way. No migration; the agents' side (what the first line means, the kits' buttons) comes with a runtime cut (deploy/first-node/RUNTIME-INTERFACE-APPS.md §5 "A note to the app's owner", §6, §7 step 9).
- The agents' figures act, and every eye is drawn (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "really don't like this needs your attention animation … along with a lot of the others, like the working animation … I want more effects and fun movements"; then, on the first pass: "I don't like the hand at all. the celebration is golden … LOOVVEEE the different animation to the eyes and the stars", and of working: "poorly rendered and looks grainy and blurry … a dead copy of the grok bot animation so would want something more of our own"). `ui/figure-acts.ts` is the arithmetic (hops with squash and stretch, leans, the drawn eyes, the symbols a figure becomes, the effects' cues) and `ui/figure-motion.ts` puts it on the page: the body's pose is a transform on the canvas about the feet, the eyes are drawn pills (or an arch for a smile) and repaint only when they change, a symbol is drawn in the figure's place as the writing dots are, and the effects are a few small shapes that fly and fade. The studio's recorded eye clips, its working stripes and their sequencer are removed (`recorded-motion.mjs`, `recorded-clips.mjs`, `eye-clips.mjs`, `working-frames.mjs`, `working-palette.mjs`, the reference sheets, `ui/working-sequence.ts`: about 2 MB), so nothing is stretched from a 160px recording, no figure fetches a sheet or reads pixels back, and the motion chunk carries the sampler and its eye tracks alone (about 140 KB where it was 950 KB). **Needs you**: the figure jumps, spins once round in the air and comes down as a symbol in its own colour, a "?" when a question waits for the member and a "!" for anything else that waits on them (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "jumps, spins and transforms into a question mark for a pending question, or … an exclamation mark for anything else needing your attention, with the exclamation point and question mark being the same color as the avatar"; `FigureOptions.ask`, from `CompanyController.waitingFor`'s new `kind`). The symbol hops or shakes every couple of seconds, and now and then turns back into the figure, which looks to both sides for the member. No badge stands beside the figure any more (`.rail-hand` and its tokens are gone). **Working**: the figure stays itself, eyes and all (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, on a pass that turned it into a spinning star: "dont like that working animation, I want to keep their avatar forms and eyes"), and juggles three small stars: it bumps one up at every beat from its shoulders in turn, bounces from side to side under them, and its eyes follow each star over the top. Every few seconds it shows a trick, and it goes through all eleven, in an order of its own, before it shows any again (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "so much better … I would like some more variance to what it can do"): a smile, a look at the member, a twirl under the stars, a round of higher throws, one star sent sky high, a header off the top of its head, a halo of them circling overhead, a "ta-da" with the stars held out, and three ways of sending the stars round the figure itself, in front of it and behind (Jonas: "could they also go around the avatar, similar to the movement the other animation did?"): a level ring, a ring that tilts as it turns, and a spiral up over its head and down. A star on the far side is under the figure's canvas (`.figure` now stacks by itself, `isolation: isolate`; `Prop.behind`, `.figure-prop.is-behind`). Between tricks its eyes do something else every phrase of four beats (Jonas: "the eye movement is a little repetitive"): over the top with each star, on one star all the way round, up at the top of the arc, on the member, or from one shoulder to the other, with a blink at a different moment each time. The stars are three small elements over the figure, each a transform written when it changes. The stars of every effect are fuller and in the vivid colours of the agents' own colour row instead of the studio's pastels, with a soft glow on a burst's stars (Jonas: "would probably like the stars and effects to be more vibrant"); a juggled star never takes the figure's own colour. A figure that works while something waits on the member catches its stars now and then, becomes its "?" or "!" for a moment, and takes them up again. A figure that writes while something waits on the member is its dots, which become the "?" or "!" for a moment every few seconds; under reduced motion a working or a writing figure with something waiting shows the symbol at rest (review r1 F1 on #1735: the badge this replaces was always in sight). The question dock, an app's ask bar, a coding session that waits for an answer and the head of a waiting card in a thread ask with the "?" too, and the card with a "!" when it is no question (F4). A figure that comes back into view shows none of the effects it missed, and a late frame sets none off (F2); a cheer asked of a figure out of view no longer holds the clock of the agent's other views, and is dropped when the figure shows too late for it (F3). **Writing**: the three dots hop in turn and land soft. **Work done** (a row that goes from working or writing to idle): the long cheer, two jumps, a twirl once round in the air and a happy rock, smiling, with stars the whole way (3.6 s; `hooray` is the short one). **Idle**: a drawn opening (a blink into a glance up); in the rail the figure blinks, looks around, smiles, stretches, sways or skips every 4 to 13 seconds, and perks up when the pointer comes over its row. A member who wants no motion sees none of it.
- A card that waits for the member is a widget that says so, in a thread (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026, on the answered card's redraw: "I still want some form of widget with proposed answers and a clear visual that indicates something to do"). A waiting card was a grey-framed box with a small-caps label row ("QUESTION · Co-Founder · 11:58 AM") and a small yellow "Waiting for you" badge, which wrapped over two lines on a phone. Now the frame is drawn in the accent colour inside a soft ring of it that breathes (only the ring's opacity moves; still under reduced motion), and the card's head is the agent's figure, waiting, beside "{name} asks you" in that colour ("{name} needs you" on a card that is no question); the label row stays for a screen reader and the card tells its time on a hover. The title is larger, the proposed answers are 38 px pills that take the accent on a hover, and the way out (Skip, Decline, Discard) stands quiet in the widget's top corner, last for the keyboard. An answer of the member's own has a round Send at the end of its field, in the composer's colour once something is typed; Enter sends as before, and a question that takes several answers keeps its one Send. Answered, the widget becomes the exchange of the entry below. A member who cannot answer the card, and the questions dock, see the card as before.
- Folding a group in the apps drawer: its apps shrink and go back into the group's tile, the nearest first, as the rest glide into place (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "missing an animation when a group is collapsing"); before, they faded where they stood under the tiles gliding over them.
- A company's time zone comes from a member's browser (Jonas, 12 Nov 2026, asked whether each company gets a time zone taken from the founder's browser so an agent's desktop clock is right: "Yes, from the browser"; migration `20260930739000`). Nothing shows in the app and there is no setting. When a company opens whose team read says it has no time zone (`time_zone` null) and the member may act for it (an owner, admin or contributor, never a viewer), the app hands in the zone it shows times in, once: the choice saved under Account, Time zone, else the device's as the browser reports it (`app/company-time-zone.ts`, `CloudApi.ensureCompanyTimeZone`, `qorpera_ensure_company_time_zone`). The cloud takes it only while the company has none and answers the company's zone, which the team then holds. That covers a new company at its first open and every existing company at a member's next visit. Nothing is handed in when the browser reports no zone, for a company that has one, or on a cloud whose team read says nothing of one (before the migration); a call that failed is not made again by that open, and a zone the cloud refused (a name it does not know) is remembered in the browser and not handed in again.
- Shared input carries the lease generation it was made under, and the server refuses it once someone held the desktop privately since (migration `20260930738000`): the authority under the window's own checks of #1701. A sharing is made under the generation the list names, every action of it sends it (`WebMachineScreens.act(machineId, action, {generation}, signal)`), and a list that names another ends the sharing and its queue, said. When the server refuses an action `DESKTOP_HELD_SINCE` (a hold taken and given back between two reads, another member's or the member's own elsewhere, which the window never saw), the queue goes, the window says "Your last input did not go through. Try again.", takes no input until a list read begun after the refusal answers, and goes on under the generation it names. A list from a database before it names none: actions go without one, as before.
- A member shares an agent's computer with it again (Jonas, 12 Nov 2026: "i no longer share control with the agent but need this take over/hand back thing? why did we regress so much since the \"office\" computers"; then "Yes, shared control"; migration `20260930734000`). The small window is watched only, with no Take over on it, and a click on its picture opens the large view (in the thread, never full screen; a press that moves still drags the window). In the large view an owner, admin or contributor clicks and types at once, with no hold, while the agent goes on working on the same screen: each action goes alone (`{"action": …}`, `WebMachineScreens.act` with no hold), one at a time. The first time in this browser one quiet line says "You and {name} share this computer: you can both click and type." until the large view is left. Who else used it in the last minute is said in the small window and the large one ("Ada is using it too.", the list's `others`). A viewer reads "You can watch. Someone with edit access can use the computer." in the large view. A desktop busy with the agent's step (or another member's, or one member's actions too fast) is waited for with "Waiting for {name} to finish a step…"; after 15 s the input is dropped and the window says "The computer was busy, so your last input did not go through. Try again." Typing privately is today's take-over, for passwords: Type privately, with a lock, the large view's one secondary control (its tip: "For passwords: {name} gets no pictures of the screen until you're done."), holds the desktop for the member alone ("Typing privately. {name} gets no pictures of the screen until you're done." and Done; in the small window "Typing privately. Choose Enter to click and type."); another member sees "{member} is typing privately. The screen is hidden until they're done."; ending it says "Ending private typing…" or "Private typing could not be ended yet. It ends by itself within 5 minutes." with Try again. Shared input goes only to the computer chosen now and only while the large view is open and no one types privately: a switch to another computer, Back, Type privately or a close ends what is on its way at once, also while a look waits (review r1 F1 of #1701). The picture's input is its own object for each sharing and each private hold, so what the picture still gathered at such a change (a text not sent yet, a click waiting for its second, a wheel turn, a press) goes to the computer and the mode it was made for or nowhere, never to another computer and never out in the other mode (review r2 F1 of #1701). Shared input stands on one predicate, in one place (`#sharing`, review r3 F1 of #1701): this window, in a thread, in the large view; a member who may act; the computer chosen now, in the agent's list, running, its screen seen with a picture since it was chosen and no look refused since; and no private hold known of it, anyone's (the list's `control`: another member's, or the member's own in another window), nor this page's (held, on its way, being given back, owed in the ledger, a give-back not confirmed). Every list read, look answer, action answer and press that can make it false ends the sharing with its queue at once, and a sharing is never used again once it was false: a new one is made when it holds again. A refusal that is not busy (`DESKTOP_BUSY`, `DESKTOP_ACTIONS_TOO_FAST`), or an action with no answer, drops the whole queue instead of trying again; the window says "Your last input did not go through. Try again." where the screen's state does not say it. Every queued input carries when it was made, and nothing older than 20 s (`INPUT_MAX_AGE_MS`: the 15 s wait on a busy desktop, itself past the host's 11 s grace, one 1 s retry and the send) is ever sent, shared or private. A sharing is also made under the member's hand-back epoch for the computer (the list's, the ledger's) and ends, its queue with it and said, when a higher one is seen: a private hold of the member's taken and given back in another window between two reads (review r4 F1 of #1701). Shared input that waited (a busy desktop's retry, or behind an action slower than 1 s) is sent only after a list read begun after the wait has answered, within 3 s, or it is dropped and said. The age of an input is from the member's own event, by the wall clock and the monotonic clock both, carried through the picture's buffers (a text batch, a click waiting for its second, a wheel burst, a held press: each as old as its first event; review r4 F2): too old by either clock, or either clock gone back since, and it is dropped and said, never sent. Joining inputs keeps every part's verdict (review r5 F1 of #1701): a text's keys, a wheel burst, a press and its release, a click and its second, and texts joined in the send queue carry the oldest and the newest time of their parts by each clock and their largest wall-minus-monotonic offset, so a joined action is too old whenever any part is, and a wall clock set back since any part counts even after it came forward again. When sharing ends without the member's doing (an epoch moved on, another member's or their own private hold elsewhere, the machine asleep or gone, a viewer), input the picture still held for it (a text not sent yet, a press not let go) is said lost once, as input in the queue was; an end of their own (a switch, Back, Type privately, a close) says nothing (review r5 F2). Input typed in one mode never goes out in the other: the shared and the private input are two inputs, and what the picture gathered goes to the one it was gathered for or nowhere; none goes while Type privately is on its way. The take-over's mechanism, its ledger and every fence are unchanged. Danish in `ui/danish/workspace.ts`; pictures `lanes/desktop-computers-20261110/shared-control/shots/`.
- Icon and colour, and a group's Colour: the colours stand in two rows of six, in the icons' columns; the panel's title and the note under a group's colours are gone, and a group's colours-only panel is narrower (Jonas, 13 Nov 2026: "polish this - it should be more than one row for the colors, and remove \"Concepts\", and \"Apps in Group take its colour, so this changes all of them.\"").
- Giving an address to an agent is one step in the Mailbox's Addresses (Jonas, 12 Nov 2026: "also don't like the UX of giving an agent an email - should be more intuitive"). Each address is on one line now, not two: its name, who answers it, how much came in the last 7 days, and Pause and Remove. Who answers it is a chip with the agent's figure; it opens the agents at work, those who answer the address ticked, and a press gives the address to an agent or takes it from one. Before, Change opened a row of agents, and a pick only asked the Co-Founder in its thread to arrange it, with nothing to see on the page. Now the page does it itself: it writes a routine of that agent's that starts on mail to the address (the member's own save, `when: event`, on their own model account that works while they are away, as a routine's editor picks it); an agent that answered it earlier takes up the routine it had last, and gets a new one when that stays paused. An address may have several agents, as the cloud lets several routines listen to a mailbox: the chip names them ("Ada and Bo"), a second agent's line says it answers the address "too", and one agent's answering never changes another's, so nothing can stop half way and leave an address with nobody. What the cloud holds after decides what is said: an agent is said to answer an address only once a routine of its own is at work, also when an answer was lost on the way.
